To address this issue, we instead edit near the data manifold, where small local updates can replace repeated re-synthesis. To enable this, we estimate a local manifold tangent space directly from perturbed samples and prove that this sample-based estimator closely approximates the true tangent. Building on this guarantee, we devise a Jacobian-free algorithm that constructs a tangent frame via small perturbations to the initial noise and alternates small tangent moves with diffusion-based projections. Updates within this frame follow principled on-manifold directions while suppressing off-manifold drift, enabling fine-grained edits without full re-diffusion or additional training. Edit strength is controlled by the number of steps for rapid, continuous adjustments that preserve fidelity and plug into existing samplers. Empirically, the resulting tangent directions yield smooth, semantic unsupervised traversals and effective CLIP-guided optimization, demonstrating practical interactive continuous editing.

</details>


### [326] [Model-Free Inference of Investor Preferences: A Relative Entropy IRL Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24280)
*Chen Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于相对熵逆强化学习（RE-IRL）的框架，用于从投资者的观测行为和市场条件中恢复其奖励函数。该框架适用于转移概率未知的环境，并通过K近邻方法解决数据稀疏性问题，同时提出了统计检验方法评估结果的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的逆强化学习算法依赖于已知的环境转移概率，但在金融市场等复杂场景中，转移概率通常是未知或难以获取的。因此，需要一种不依赖转移概率的IRL方法（RE-IRL）来恢复投资者的奖励函数，从而理解其决策动机。

Method: 1. 采用相对熵逆强化学习（RE-IRL）框架，适用于转移概率未知的环境。2. 使用K近邻方法估计观测到的行为策略，以应对数据稀疏性问题。3. 构建统计检验框架，评估估计结果的效度和鲁棒性。

Result: 成功从观测的投资行为和市场条件中恢复了投资者的奖励函数，并通过统计检验验证了结果的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RE-IRL框架结合K近邻方法和统计检验，能够有效且鲁棒地从稀疏的投资数据中恢复投资者的奖励函数，为理解金融决策行为提供了一种新工具。

Abstract: We present a framework using Relative Entropy Inverse Reinforcement Learning (RE-IRL) to recover investor reward functions from observed investment actions and market conditions. Unlike traditional IRL algorithms, RE-IRL is employed to account for environments where transition probabilities are unknown or inaccessible. To address the challenge of data sparsity, we utilize a $K$-nearest neighbor approach to estimate the observed behavior policy. Furthermore, we propose a statistical testing framework to evaluate the validity and robustness of the estimated results.

</details>


### [327] [Latent-Hysteresis Graph ODEs: Modeling Coupled Topology-Feature Evolution via Continuous Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24293)
*Qinhan Hou,Jing Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对图神经常微分方程（Graph ODEs）在严格正不可约混合算子下存在信息泄漏并收敛到全局共识吸引子的单稳态陷阱问题，提出滞后图ODE（HGODE），通过耦合特征演化与可学习的双阱势能边拓扑动力学，实现边状态的可微分两极分化，在理论和实验上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示Graph ODEs在严格正不可约混合算子下固有的单稳态陷阱：长期演化中信息必然泄漏并收敛到单一全局共识吸引子，表明现有方法在长时间传播中会丧失表示多样性。

Method: 提出Hysteresis Graph ODE (HGODE)，将特征演化与潜在拓扑势耦合，通过可学习的成对力驱动；采用双阱边势能和双极化门控机制，使边状态可微地极化为连通或绝缘相。

Result: 理论分析证明了坍塌机制及滞后拓扑动力学，并在合成诊断数据和真实图基准上验证了性能。

Conclusion: HGODE通过引入可学习的滞后拓扑动力学有效解决了Graph ODEs的单稳态信息泄漏问题，为连续深度图学习提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Graph neural ordinary differential equations (Graph ODEs) extend graph learning from discrete message-passing layers to continuous-time representation flows. While it supports adaptive long-range propagation, we show that Graph ODEs with strictly positive irreducible mixing operators face an inherent \emph{monostability trap}: in the long-time regime, information leakage is unavoidable and the dynamics converge to a single global consensus attractor. We propose the \textbf{Hysteresis Graph ODE (HGODE)}, which couples feature evolution with a latent topological potential driven by a learned pairwise force. A double-well edge potential and bipolarized gate allow edge states to polarize into connected or insulated phases while preserving differentiability. We provide asymptotic analysis of the collapse mechanism and the proposed hysteretic topology dynamics, and validate HGODE on theory-driven synthetic diagnostics and real-world graph benchmarks.

</details>


### [328] [SolarTformer: A Transformer Based Deep Learning Approach for Short Term Solar Power Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24306)
*Ankan Basu,Jyotiraditya Roy,Aditya Datta,Prayas Sanyal,Sumanta Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力机制（Transformer架构）的太阳能短期功率预测模型SolarTformer，通过自注意力机制捕捉时间依赖性和空间变异性，并引入电站元数据提升跨电站、跨季节的泛化能力，在晴天和阴天均表现出优于传统模型的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳能功率的准确预测对于可再生能源高效并网至关重要，传统模型在捕捉时间依赖性和空间变异性方面存在不足，亟需更鲁棒、泛化能力更强的预测方法。

Method: 基于Transformer架构构建SolarTformer模型，采用自注意力机制提取太阳辐照度的时间依赖性与空间变异性，同时将电站特定元数据（如地理位置、面板配置、季节信息）作为输入特征，增强模型在不同电站间的泛化能力。

Result: 在相同数据集上，SolarTformer显著优于此前模型，且在晴天和阴天条件下均表现强劲，展现出高鲁棒性和泛化性。

Conclusion: 基于注意力的架构在提升太阳能预测准确性方面具有巨大潜力，有助于实现更可靠的可再生能源管理。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of solar power output is essential for efficient integration of renewable energy into the grid. In this study, an attention-based deep learning model, inspired by transformer architecture, is used for short-term solar power forecasting. Our proposed model, "SolarTformer", is designed to predict solar power output from meteorological data. Unlike traditional models, SolarTformer leverages self-attention mechanisms to effectively capture temporal dependencies and spatial variability in solar irradiance. In addition, the proposed methodology includes feeding power station-specific metadata into the model, which helps to generalize between power stations located at different locations and with different panel configurations and in different seasons. Our experiments demonstrate that SolarTformer significantly outperforms previous models on the same data set. In particular, the model exhibits strong performance on both clear and cloudy days, indicating high robustness and generalizability. These findings highlight the potential of attention-based architectures in enhancing the accuracy of solar forecasting, contributing to a more reliable management of renewable energy.

</details>


### [329] [Mitigating Error Amplification in Fast Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24332)
*Mengnan Zhao,Lihe Zhang,Bo Wang,Tianhang Zheng,Hong Zhong,Geyong Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分布感知动态引导（DDG）策略，通过根据样本置信度动态调整扰动幅度和监督信号，同时引入加权正则化约束，有效缓解了快速对抗训练中的灾难性过拟合和鲁棒性-准确性权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速对抗训练（FAT）在提升模型鲁棒性方面有效，但存在两个关键问题：灾难性过拟合（CO）和鲁棒性-准确性权衡（干净输入性能下降）。现有方法未能有效解决这些矛盾，亟需深入分析其根本原因并提出针对性解决方案。

Method: 1) 对不同置信度组进行扰动和监督水平的全面分析，发现低置信度样本是CO和鲁棒性-准确性权衡的主要来源。2) 提出DDG策略：根据样本在真实类别上的置信度动态调整扰动幅度，引导样本走向一致性决策边界；根据样本预测状态动态调整监督信号，避免过度强调错误信号。3) 设计加权正则化约束缓解动态引导带来的梯度不稳定性。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上的大量实验表明，DDG有效缓解了灾难性过拟合和鲁棒性-准确性权衡问题。

Conclusion: 通过对置信度分组的深入分析，揭示了低置信度样本在对抗训练中的关键作用，所提出的DDG策略通过动态引导机制有效平衡了鲁棒性与准确性，为快速对抗训练提供了一种新的解决思路。

Abstract: Fast Adversarial Training (FAT) has proven effective in enhancing model robustness by encouraging networks to learn perturbation-invariant representations. However, FAT often suffers from catastrophic overfitting (CO), where the model overfits to the training attack and fails to generalize to unseen ones. Moreover, robustness oriented optimization typically leads to notable performance degradation on clean inputs, and such degradation becomes increasingly severe as the perturbation budget grows. In this work, we conduct a comprehensive analysis of how guidance strength affects model performance by modulating perturbation and supervision levels across distinct confidence groups. The findings reveal that low confidence samples are the primary contributors to CO and the robustness accuracy trade off. Building on this insight, we propose a Distribution-aware Dynamic Guidance (DDG) strategy that dynamically adjusts both the perturbation budget and supervision signal. Specifically, DDG scales the perturbation magnitude according to the sample confidence at the ground truth class, thereby guiding samples toward consistent decision boundaries while mitigating the influence of learning spurious correlations. Simultaneously, it dynamically adjusts the supervision signal based on the prediction state of each sample, preventing overemphasis on incorrect signals. To alleviate potential gradient instability arising from dynamic guidance, we further design a weighted regularization constraint. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that DDG effectively alleviates both CO and the robustness accuracy trade off.

</details>


### [330] [Perfecting Aircraft Maneuvers with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24338)
*Atahan Cilan,Mahir Demir,Özgün Can Yürütken,Seyyid Osman Sevgili,Ümit Can Bekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于AI的先进喷气教练机特技机动，旨在开发AI辅助飞行员训练模块，使用强化学习智能体模拟多种飞机机动作为未来飞行员的训练工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发AI辅助飞行员训练模块，提高特定飞机机动的训练效果。

Method: 使用强化学习（RL）智能体模拟多种飞机特技机动。

Result: 成功利用RL智能体模拟了多种飞机机动，可作为训练工具。

Conclusion: 基于AI的强化学习智能体能够有效模拟飞机机动，为未来飞行员提供辅助训练工具。

Abstract: This paper evaluates an advanced jet trainer's utilization of artificial intelligence (AI)-based aircraft aerobatic maneuvers with the intention of developing an AI-assisted pilot training module for specific aircraft maneuvers. A multitude of aircraft maneuvers have been simulated using reinforcement learning (RL) agents, which will serve as a training tool for future pilots.

</details>


### [331] [Unveiling the Backdoor Mechanism Hidden Behind Catastrophic Overfitting in Fast Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24350)
*Mengnan Zhao,Lihe Zhang,Tianhang Zheng,Bo Wang,Baocai Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文创新性地从后门攻击视角解释快速对抗训练中的灾难性过拟合现象，将其与后门攻击和不可学习任务统一于同一理论框架，并提出了基于后门启发策略的缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对灾难性过拟合缺乏系统且直观的解释，需要一个统一的理论框架来理解CO的本质。

Method: 通过路径划分、多样特征预测和通用类别可区分触发器的验证，将CO概念化为不可学习任务的弱触发变体；提出基于后门启发的缓解策略：微调/线性探测/重初始化校准参数，以及权重异常值抑制约束。

Result: 实验支持对CO的后门视角解释，验证了所提缓解策略的有效性。

Conclusion: CO、后门攻击和不可学习任务可统一于共同的理论框架，后门启发策略能有效缓解CO。

Abstract: Fast Adversarial Training (FAT) has attracted significant attention due to its efficiency in enhancing neural network robustness against adversarial attacks. However, FAT is prone to catastrophic overfitting (CO), wherein models overfit to the specific attack used during training and fail to generalize to others. While existing methods introduce diverse hypotheses and propose various strategies to mitigate CO, a systematic and intuitive explanation of CO remains absent. In this work, we innovatively interpret CO through the lens of backdoor. Through validations on pathway division, diverse feature predictions, and universal class distinguishable triggers in CO, we conceptualize CO as a weak trigger variant of unlearnable tasks, unifying CO, backdoor attacks, and unlearnable tasks under a common theoretical framework. Guided by this, we leverage several backdoor inspired strategies to mitigate CO: (i) Recalibrate CO affected model parameters using vanilla fine tuning, linear probing, or reinitialization-based techniques; (ii) Introduce a weight outlier suppression constraint to regulate abnormal deviations in model weights. Extensive experiments support our interpretation of CO and show the efficacy of the proposed mitigation strategies.

</details>


### [332] [An Aircraft Upset Recovery System with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24355)
*Mahir Demir,Atahan Cilan,Seyyid Osman Sevgili,Özgün Can Yürütken,Ümit Can Bekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了基于人工智能的飞行员激活恢复系统（PARS）在高级喷气式教练机中的应用，采用软演员-评论家（SAC）强化学习架构与超参数优化，融合负G惩罚等专家特征，经评估其性能优于传统控制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升高级喷气式教练机飞行员激活恢复系统的运行效率，利用AI技术弥补传统控制方法的不足。

Method: 采用先进强化学习架构（软演员-评论家SAC模型），结合超参数优化方法，并融入控制工程师和领域专家关注的负G惩罚等手工特征。

Result: 经专家评估，AI模型的行为比传统控制方法更优，表现更令人满意。

Conclusion: 基于AI的PARS系统在高级喷气式教练机中展现出优于传统控制的性能，具有提升操作效率的潜力。

Abstract: This article explores the progress made in the creation of a pilot activated recovery system (PARS) for advanced jet trainers that utilizes artificial intelligence (AI) in an effort to enhance operational efficiency. The PARS model employs an advanced reinforcement learning (RL) architecture, incorporating a cutting-edge soft-actor critic (SAC) model and hyper-parameter optimization methods. Negative-g punishments and other handcrafted features remarked upon by control engineers and domain experts regarding PARS are also taken into account by the system. When evaluated by them, the AI model's behavior is deemed more desirable than that of conventional control methods.

</details>


### [333] [DPRM: A Plug-in Doob h transform-induced Token-Ordering Module for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24357)
*Dake Bu,Wei Huang,Andi Han,Hau-San Wong,Qingfu Zhang,Taiji Suzuki,Atsushi Nitanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPRM是一种即插即用的令牌排序模块，通过在线估计从置信度驱动逐步过渡到Doob h变换过程奖励引导的排序策略，在保持扩散语言模型架构不变的情况下提升生成质量，在预训练、后训练、测试时缩放和单细胞掩码扩散等多个场景中优于置信度基线，尤其在困难推理子集上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型的令牌排序策略存在缺陷：随机掩码导致训练-测试不匹配，置信度驱动规则虽高效但目光短浅且抑制探索。需要一种更好的排序策略来提升模型性能。

Method: 提出DPRM模块，保持宿主架构、去噪目标和监督不变，仅改变排序策略。从置信度驱动的渐进排序开始，通过在线估计逐步过渡到Doob h变换过程奖励引导的排序。理论特征化为奖励倾斜的Gibbs揭示律，证明了阶段Soft-BoN近似的O(1/N)收敛性，以及在线桶控制器的经验-伯恩斯坦率跟踪。

Result: 在预训练、后训练、测试时缩放和单细胞掩码扩散任务上优于置信度基线，困难推理子集上提升尤为显著。在蛋白质、分子生成和DNA设计任务上，排序感知变体在特定结构或片段约束指标上有显著改进，但并非在所有质量指标上均一致优于基线。

Conclusion: 令牌排序是扩散语言模型的一个基础控制轴，DPRM是一种通用的改进模块。代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion language models generate without a fixed left-to-right order, making token ordering a central algorithmic choice: which tokens should be revealed, retained, revised or verified at each step? Existing systems mainly use random masking or confidence-driven ordering. Random masking creates train--test mismatch, while confidence-only rules are efficient but can be myopic and suppress useful exploration.
  We introduce DPRM (Doob h-transform Process Reward Model), a plug-in token-ordering module for diffusion language models. DPRM keeps the host architecture, denoising objective and supervision unchanged, and changes only the ordering policy. It starts from confidence-driven progressive ordering and gradually shifts to Doob h transform Process Reward guided ordering through online estimates.
  We characterize the exact DPRM policy as a reward-tilted Gibbs reveal law, prove O(1/N) convergence of the stagewise Soft-BoN approximation, and show that the online bucketized controller tracks the exact DPRM score at empirical-Bernstein rates. Under tractable optimization assumptions, DPRM also yields a sample-complexity advantage over random and confidence-only ordering.
  DPRM improves over confidence-based baselines in pretraining, post-training, test-time scaling, and single-cell masked diffusion, with particularly strong gains on harder reasoning subsets. In protein, molecular generation and DNA design, the effect is more multi-objective: ordering-aware variants significantly improve selected structural or fragment-constrained metrics while not uniformly dominating the host baseline on every quality metric. These results identify token ordering as a fundamental control axis in diffusion language models and establish DPRM as a general-purpose module for improving it. Code is available at https://github.com/DakeBU/DPRM-DLLM.

</details>


### [334] [SAGE: Sparse Adaptive Guidance for Dependency-Aware Tabular Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24368)
*Shuo Yang,Zheyu Zhang,Bardh Prenkaj,Gjergji Kasneci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGE是一种基于LLM的新型框架，通过稀疏自适应依赖图生成高保真合成表格数据，相比传统LLM方法在F1分数上提升10%，同时减少策略违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的表格数据生成方法存在两个根本缺陷：(1)密集建模特征依赖关系，引入虚假相关性；(2)假设特征间关系是静态的，忽略了依赖关系随特征值变化的情况。

Method: SAGE将特征离散化为值感知的伪特征，构建基于互信息的稀疏依赖图，通过显式上下文选择或隐式logit校正自适应地指导生成过程，使LLM在合成时聚焦于真正相关的信息。

Result: 在六个数据集上的多个任务中，SAGE比此前LLM方法在F1分数上提升10%，并将策略违规减少1个点。

Conclusion: SAGE证明了自适应结构在表格数据生成中的重要性，并为LLM的上下文敏感控制提供了新见解。

Abstract: Generating high-fidelity synthetic tabular data remains a critical challenge for enhancing data availability in privacy-sensitive and low-resource domains. Recent approaches leverage LLMs by representing table rows as sequences, yet suffer from two fundamental limitations: (1) they model feature dependencies densely, introducing spurious correlations; and (2) they assume static relationships between features, ignoring how these dependencies vary with feature values. To overcome these limitations, we introduce SAGE (Sparse Adaptive Guidance), a novel LLM-based generation framework that enforces sparse and dynamic dependency guidance. SAGE discretizes features into value-aware pseudo-features and constructs a mutual information-based sparse dependency graph. This graph adaptively guides generation through explicit context selection or implicit logit correction, enabling LLMs to focus on truly relevant information during synthesis. Our extensive experiments across six datasets and multiple tasks reveal that SAGE not only improves data fidelity and downstream utility, boosting F1 scores by 10% compared to previous LLM-based methods, but also reduces policy violations by one point. These results highlight the importance of adaptive structure in tabular data generation and provide new insights into context-sensitive control of LLMs.

</details>


### [335] [Advancing Ligand-based Virtual Screening and Molecular Generation with Pretrained Molecular Embedding Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24474)
*Shiyun Wa,Yifei Wang,Simone Sciabola,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出预训练嵌入距离（PED），作为一种无需任务特定训练即可从预训练分子模型中直接计算的新型分子相似度度量，在虚拟筛选和分子生成中表现有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分子相似度度量（如指纹Tanimoto系数、3D形状叠加）在大规模计算时昂贵或依赖手工描述符，而现有深度学习方法需要相似性特定监督或昂贵的数据整理，缺乏通用性。

Method: 直接从预训练分子模型中计算嵌入距离（PED），无需任务特定训练，将其作为分子相似度度量。

Result: PED与传统相似度度量呈现不同的相关性，在虚拟筛选的分子排序和通过奖励设计引导分子生成方面均表现有效。

Conclusion: 预训练分子嵌入能够捕获丰富的结构信息，可作为现代AI辅助药物发现中一种有前景且可扩展的相似度度量方法。

Abstract: Molecular similarity plays a central role in ligand-based drug discovery, such as virtual screening, analog searching, and goal-directed molecular generation. However, traditional similarity measures, ranging from fingerprint-based Tanimoto coefficients to 3D shape overlays, are often computationally expensive at scale or rely on hand-crafted molecular descriptors. Meanwhile, many deep learning approaches to similarity-aware design still depend on similarity-specific supervision or costly data curation, limiting their generality across targets. In this work, we propose pretrained embedding distance (PED) as an effective alternative, computed directly from pretrained molecular models without task-specific training. Experimental results show that PED exhibits distinct correlations with traditional similarity metrics, and performs effectively in both ranking molecules for virtual screening and guiding molecular generation via reward design. These findings suggest that pretrained molecular embeddings capture rich structural information and can serve as a promising and scalable similarity measurement for modern AI-aided drug discovery.

</details>


### [336] [SceneSelect: Selective Learning for Trajectory Scene Classification and Expert Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24514)
*Xinrun Wang,Deshun Xia,Ke Xu,Weijie Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SceneSelect的场景中心轨迹预测范式，通过无监督聚类发现场景类别并动态路由输入到最合适的专家模型，在三个公开基准上平均提升10.5%，且无需联合重训练即可集成现有模型并适应新数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型中心范式使用单一固定容量模型应对高度异构的真实场景（运动速度、空间密度、交互模式差异大），导致泛化差距、精度下降和计算浪费，亟需一种场景自适应的新范式。

Method: 提出场景中心的选择性学习范式SceneSelect：(1) 对可解释几何与运动学特征进行无监督聚类，发现潜在场景分类体系；(2) 训练高度解耦的分类模块将实时输入分配到对应场景类别；(3) 采用即插即用的调度策略将轨迹序列路由到最优专家预测器。解耦设计支持与现成模型无缝集成且无需联合重训练。

Result: 在ETH-UCY、SDD和NBA三个公开基准上，SceneSelect一致优于强单模型和集成基线，平均提升10.5%。

Conclusion: 场景感知的选择性学习能够有效克服轨迹预测中的场景异构性挑战，解耦设计保证了出色的泛化能力和可扩展性。

Abstract: Accurate trajectory prediction is fundamentally challenging due to high scene heterogeneity - the severe variance in motion velocity, spatial density, and interaction patterns across different real-world environments. However, most existing approaches typically train a single unified model, expecting a fixed-capacity architecture to generalize universally across all possible scenarios. This conventional model-centric paradigm is fundamentally flawed when confronting such extreme heterogeneity, inevitably leading to a severe generalization gap, degraded accuracy, and massive computational waste. To overcome this bottleneck, rather than refining restricted model-centric architectures, we propose selective learning, a novel scene-centric paradigm. It explicitly analyzes the characteristics of the underlying scene to dynamically route inputs to the most appropriate expert models. As a concrete implementation of this paradigm, we introduce SceneSelect. Specifically, SceneSelect utilizes unsupervised clustering on interpretable geometric and kinematic features to discover a latent scene taxonomy. A highly decoupled classification module is then trained to assign real-time inputs to these scene categories, and a highly extensible, plug-and-play scheduling policy automatically dispatches the trajectory sequence to the optimal expert predictor. Crucially, this decoupled design ensures excellent generalization capabilities, allowing seamless integration with different off-the-shelf models and robust adaptation across new datasets without requiring computationally expensive joint retraining. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks (ETH-UCY, SDD, and NBA) demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms strong single-model and ensemble baselines, achieving an average improvement of 10.5%, showcasing the effectiveness of scene-aware selective learning.

</details>


### [337] [Prior-Agnostic Robust Forecast Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24517)
*Zhi Chen,Cheng Peng,Wei Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究先验无关的鲁棒预测聚合问题，允许未知状态空间（状态值在[0,1]中任意取值），提出了一种简单的闭式log-odds聚合器，并在三种知识体系下提供了（近乎）紧的极小极大遗憾保证。在条件独立结构下，未知状态空间比已知状态空间更难，聚合器可实现最差情况遗憾0.0255；在已知状态空间{0,1}下，该聚合器首次实现了严格低于0.0226的遗憾上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒预测聚合研究大多假设已知二元状态空间{0,1}，但实际中状态值可能是任意数值且聚合者对状态空间一无所知。本文旨在解决先验无关且状态空间未知下的鲁棒预测聚合问题，设计一个在多种知识体系下都能保证良好最差情况性能的通用聚合器。

Method: 提出一个简单的闭式log-odds聚合器（在logit空间线性池化预测），并在三种知识体系下推导极小极大遗憾界：（1）条件独立信号结构，（2）Blackwell序贯结构，（3）一般信息结构。同时考虑扩展模型，即聚合者已知每位专家的边际预测分布。

Result: 在未知状态空间的条件独立信号结构下，给出下界证明鲁棒聚合比已知状态空间更困难，聚合器实现最差情况遗憾0.0255；在已知状态空间{0,1}的条件独立结构下，首次实现严格低于0.0226的遗憾上界；在已知边际分布的条件独立结构下，遗憾为0.0228（下界0.0225）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的log-odds聚合器是首个能在已知状态空间{0,1}下实现严格低于0.0226遗憾上界的显式闭式聚合器，并且能够推广到未知状态空间和不同知识体系，填补了相关理论空白。

Abstract: Robust forecast aggregation combines the predictions of multiple information sources to perform well in the worst case across all possible information structures. Previous work largely focuses on settings with a known binary state space, where the state is either 0 or 1. We study prior-agnostic robust forecast aggregation in which the aggregator observes only experts' reports, yet is ignorant of both the underlying joint information structure and the full prior, including the underlying state space. Unlike the standard model that fixes the binary state space {0, 1}, we allow the (binary) unknown state values to be arbitrary numbers in [0, 1], so the same reported probability may correspond to very different realized outcome frequencies across environments.
  Our main contribution is a simple, explicit, closed-form log-odds aggregator that linearly pools forecasts in logit space, together with (nearly-)tight minimax-regret guarantees across three knowledge regimes. We first show that under conditionally independent (CI) signals, robust aggregation with an unknown state space is strictly harder than in the known-state setting by establishing a larger lower bound, and our aggregation rule can achieve a worst-case regret of 0.0255. Along the way, we also characterize tight regret bounds for Blackwell-ordered structures and for general information structures. In the classical setting with known state space {0,1}, our aggregator achieves regret strictly below 0.0226 for CI structures. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first explicit closed-form aggregator that achieves a regret upper bound strictly less than 0.0226. Finally, we extend the model where the aggregator additionally knows each expert's marginal forecast distribution; in this setting, with the CI structures, we show that a generalized log-odds rule achieves regret of 0.0228, complementing with a lower bound of 0.0225.

</details>


### [338] [Stochastic simultaneous optimistic optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24537)
*Michal Valko,Alexandra Carpentier,Rémi Munos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StoSOO是一种无需知道函数局部平滑性先验知识的全局优化算法，在仅有有限次含噪评估的条件下，通过乐观策略构建层次划分上的上置信界来迭代采样，其性能几乎与知悉平滑性的最优调参算法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于bandit的全局优化方法（如Kleinberg et al., 2008; Bubeck et al., 2011a）需要预先知道函数关于某个半度量的局部光滑性信息，而这一知识在实际应用中往往不可获得。本文旨在设计一种不依赖该先验知识的算法，同时保持与最优化算法相近的性能。

Method: 提出StoSOO算法，采用乐观策略（optimistic strategy），在函数域的层次化划分上迭代地构建上置信界（upper confidence bounds），据此决定下一个采样点。算法无需知道函数关于半度量的局部平滑性，仅假设函数在其某个全局最大值附近具有局部平滑性。

Result: 有限时间分析（finite-time analysis）表明，即使不知道函数的局部平滑性，StoSOO的性能几乎与专门针对已知平滑性调优的最佳算法相当。

Conclusion: StoSOO在非常弱的假设（仅需全局最大值附近局部平滑）下，成功消除了对半度量先验知识的依赖，同时保持了近乎最优的收敛性能，是对带噪全局优化问题的一项重要推进。

Abstract: We study the problem of global maximization of a function f given a finite number of evaluations perturbed by noise. We consider a very weak assumption on the function, namely that it is locally smooth (in some precise sense) with respect to some semi-metric, around one of its global maxima. Compared to previous works on bandits in general spaces (Kleinberg et al., 2008; Bubeck et al., 2011a) our algorithm does not require the knowledge of this semi-metric. Our algorithm, StoSOO, follows an optimistic strategy to iteratively construct upper confidence bounds over the hierarchical partitions of the function domain to decide which point to sample next. A finite-time analysis of StoSOO shows that it performs almost as well as the best specifically-tuned algorithms even though the local smoothness of the function is not known.

</details>


### [339] [Dialysis Risk Prediction and Treatment Effect Estimation for AKI patients using Longitudinal Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24547)
*Kalyani P. Pande,Evan Yang,Bryan Zhu,Sandeep K. Mallipattu,Alisa Yurovsky,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于EHR数据构建固定窗口队列，使用Transformer因果多头模型预测透析/ESRD风险，评估药物暴露的平均治疗效果，发现ACE/ARB具有部分保护作用而袢利尿剂呈恶化信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透析或终末期肾病是罕见但临床重要的结局，临床医生需要了解药物暴露如何影响下游风险，但目前缺乏系统的因果推断方法。

Method: 构建固定窗口EHR队列（90天观察期，730天预测期；N=81401；患病率1.1%），建模诊断、手术、用药序列及肾脏实验室指标趋势，使用基于Transformer的因果多头模型，通过反事实暴露移除与插入估计药物和成分水平的平均治疗效果，并采用IPTW、AIPW等方法进行事后因果分析验证方向性。

Result: 测试集AUC为0.694，PR-AUC为0.094；在决策阈值0.883下F1为0.201，Brier分数0.018。因果分析显示ACE/ARB暴露有部分保护方向支持，袢利尿剂暴露呈现恶化方向信号。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了利用EHR数据和因果深度学习模型评估罕见但重要临床结局药物效应的可行性，验证了ACE/ARB的肾保护作用和袢利尿剂的潜在肾损害风险。

Abstract: Progression to dialysis or end-stage renal disease is a rare but clinically important outcome. Clinicians need evidence on how medication exposures influence downstream risk. We constructed a fixed-window EHR cohort (90-day observation, 730-day prediction; N=81401; dialysis/ESRD prevalence: 1.1%) and modeled sequences of diagnoses, procedures, and medications with kidney laboratory trends (creatinine, BUN, eGFR). A transformer-based causal multi-head model was trained to estimate drug- and ingredient-level average treatment effects (ATEs) using counterfactual exposure removal and insertion under a full medication history setup. On test set, predictive performance reached an AUC of 0.694 and PR-AUC of 0.094. At the selected decision threshold (0.883), the model achieved an F1 score of 0.201 with a Brier score of 0.018. Post-hoc causal analyses of lab changes (eGFR, creatinine, BUN) using IPTW, AIPW, naive, and covariate-adjusted OLS methods assessed clinical directionality. Results showed partial protective-direction support for ACE/ARB exposures and worsening-direction signals for loop diuretics.

</details>


### [340] [Efficient learning by implicit exploration in bandit problems with side observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24555)
*Tomas Kocak,Gergely Neu,Michal Valko,Remi Munos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对部分可观测的在线学习问题（介于完全信息与赌博机反馈之间），提出了一种无需预先知晓观测系统即可获得近最优遗憾保证的隐式探索算法，并将其扩展至组合优化场景，在计算和信息效率上均优于以往策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线学习研究多聚焦于完全信息反馈或赌博机反馈两种极端，但实际应用中存在大量介于两者之间的部分可观测情形（如观测损失依赖于所选动作和环境指定的有向观测系统）。已有方法或需提前知晓观测系统结构，或计算效率低下，缺乏通用且高效的解决方案。

Method: 1. 提出**隐式探索（Implicit Exploration）**策略，在不预先知道观测系统结构的情况下，通过自适应地平衡探索与利用实现近最优遗憾。2. 针对组合优化场景，设计第二个算法以解决首个算法计算效率不足的问题，其采用更复杂的调参机制但始终可高效计算。

Result: 两个算法在部分可观测的在线学习问题中均取得了近最优的遗憾界，且隐式探索策略在计算复杂度和信息论效率上显著优于先前研究的探索策略。

Conclusion: 隐式探索是一种更高效（计算与信息双维度）的通用探索框架，成功解决了在未知观测系统下实现近最优在线学习的核心难题，并为介于半赌博机和完全信息之间的组合优化问题提供了实用解法。

Abstract: We consider online learning problems under a partial observability model capturing situations where the information conveyed to the learner is between full information and bandit feedback. In the simplest variant, we assume that in addition to its own loss, the learner also gets to observe losses of some other actions. The revealed losses depend on the learner's action and a directed observation system chosen by the environment. For this setting, we propose the first algorithm that enjoys near-optimal regret guarantees without having to know the observation system before selecting its actions. Along similar lines, we also define a new partial information setting that models online combinatorial optimization problems where the feedback received by the learner is between semi-bandit and full feedback. As the predictions of our first algorithm cannot be always computed efficiently in this setting, we propose another algorithm with similar properties and with the benefit of always being computationally efficient, at the price of a slightly more complicated tuning mechanism. Both algorithms rely on a novel exploration strategy called implicit exploration, which is shown to be more efficient both computationally and information-theoretically than previously studied exploration strategies for the problem.

</details>


### [341] [Uncovering Latent Patterns in Social Media Usage and Mental Health: A Clustering-Based Approach Using Unsupervised Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24611)
*Md All Shahria,Sanjeda Dewan Mithila,Touhid Alam,Mohammad Sakib Mahmood,Mahfuza Khatun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过K-Means聚类（6簇，轮廓系数0.32）对551名参与者的社交媒体使用与心理健康数据进行分析，发现社交媒体使用时长与焦虑呈0.28正相关，揭示了不同行为-心理模式下的风险特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体普及引发对其心理影响的关注，但现有研究多依赖相关性分析，缺乏利用无监督学习对用户进行行为-心理模式细分的方法，无法识别不同群体的差异化风险特征。

Method: 收集551名参与者在线调查数据，采用KNN插补处理缺失值、独热编码处理分类变量（如性别，5个唯一值）、IQR与Z-score检测异常值；使用肘部法和轮廓系数（0.32）确定最优聚类数为6，应用K-Means聚类，并通过PCA将22维降至2维进行可视化，辅以相关性热力图分析。

Result: 最优聚类数为6（轮廓系数0.32），PCA将22维降至2维实现可视化，相关性分析显示社交媒体使用时长与焦虑呈0.28的正相关。

Conclusion: 无监督学习方法能有效识别社交媒体用户的不同行为-心理亚群，为针对性干预提供依据。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of social media has heightened interest in its psychological effects, particularly on mental health indicators such as anxiety, depression, loneliness, and sleep quality, as these platforms increasingly influence social interactions and well-being. Although previous research has examined correlations between social media use and mental health, few studies have utilized unsupervised machine learning to segment users based on behavioral and psychological patterns, leaving a gap in identifying distinct risk profiles across diverse groups. This study seeks to address this by segmenting individuals according to their social media usage and psychological well-being, employing clustering to reveal hidden patterns and evaluate their mental health implications. Data from 551 participants, collected via an online survey, were preprocessed using KNN imputation for missing values, one-hot encoding for categorical variables like Gender with 5 unique values, and outlier detection via IQR and Z-score methods. K-Means clustering, optimized at 6 clusters using the Elbow Method and a Silhouette Score of 0.32, was applied, with PCA reducing 22 dimensions for visualization and a correlation heatmap highlighting relationships, such as a 0.28 correlation between social media hours and anxiety.

</details>


### [342] [The Last Human-Written Paper: Agent-Native Research Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24658)
*Jiachen Liu,Jiaxin Pei,Jintao Huang,Chenglei Si,Ao Qu,Xiangru Tang,Runyu Lu,Lichang Chen,Xiaoyan Bai,Haizhong Zheng,Carl Chen,Zhiyang Chen,Haojie Ye,Yujuan Fu,Zexue He,Zijian Jin,Zhenyu Zhang,Shangquan Sun,Maestro Harmon,John Dianzhuo Wang,Jianqiao Zeng,Jiachen Sun,Mingyuan Wu,Baoyu Zhou,Yuchen You,Shijian Lu,Yiming Qiu,Fan Lai,Yuan Yuan,Yao Li,Junyuan Hong,Ruihao Zhu,Beidi Chen,Alex Pentland,Ang Chen,Mosharaf Chowdhury,Zechen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent-Native Research Artifact (Ara)协议，用四层机器可执行结构替代传统线性叙事论文，保留失败实验和实现细节，将问答准确率从72.4%提升至93.7%，复现成功率从57.4%提升至64.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学论文将分支迭代的研究过程压缩为线性叙事，导致两种结构性成本：一是"叙事税"（丢弃失败实验和被拒假设），二是"工程税"（实现细节缺失）。当AI Agent需要理解、复现和扩展已有研究时，这些成本变得关键。

Method: 提出Ara协议，包含四层结构：科学逻辑、可执行代码及完整规范、保留失败记录的探索图、将每条结论关联原始输出的证据层。配套三个机制：Live Research Manager在开发中捕获决策和死路；Ara Compiler将传统PDF和仓库转换为Ara；Ara原生审稿系统自动化客观检查。

Result: 在PaperBench上将问答准确率从72.4%提升至93.7%；在RE-Bench上将复现成功率从57.4%提升至64.4%。保留的失败轨迹能加速扩展任务，但也可能限制强Agent跳出原有探索范围。

Conclusion: Ara协议能显著提升AI Agent对科研工作的理解和复现能力，但需注意失败轨迹的双刃剑效应：对能力较弱的Agent有帮助，对能力强的Agent可能形成路径依赖。

Abstract: Scientific publication compresses a branching, iterative research process into a linear narrative, discarding the majority of what was discovered along the way. This compilation imposes two structural costs: a Storytelling Tax, where failed experiments, rejected hypotheses, and the branching exploration process are discarded to fit a linear narrative; and an Engineering Tax, where the gap between reviewer-sufficient prose and agent-sufficient specification leaves critical implementation details unwritten. Tolerable for human readers, these costs become critical when AI agents must understand, reproduce, and extend published work. We introduce the Agent-Native Research Artifact (Ara), a protocol that replaces the narrative paper with a machine-executable research package structured around four layers: scientific logic, executable code with full specifications, an exploration graph that preserves the failures compilation discards, and evidence grounding every claim in raw outputs. Three mechanisms support the ecosystem: a Live Research Manager that captures decisions and dead ends during ordinary development; an Ara Compiler that translates legacy PDFs and repos into Aras; and an Ara-native review system that automates objective checks so human reviewers can focus on significance, novelty, and taste. On PaperBench and RE-Bench, Ara raises question-answering accuracy from 72.4% to 93.7% and reproduction success from 57.4% to 64.4%. On RE-Bench's five open-ended extension tasks, preserved failure traces in Ara accelerate progress, but can also constrain a capable agent from stepping outside the prior-run box depending on the agent's capabilities.

</details>


### [343] [Diffusion-Guided Feature Selection via Nishimori Temperature: Noise-Based Spectral Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24692)
*Vasiliy S. Usatyuk,Denis A. Sapozhnikov,Sergey I. Egorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于噪声的谱嵌入（NBSE）框架，通过物理启发的Nishimori温度和Bethe Hessian矩阵实现高维数据无贪心特征选择，在ImageNet嵌入上仅用30%特征即可保持分类精度下降低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征选择方法多依赖贪心搜索，计算成本高且易陷入局部最优；需要一种无需贪心搜索、鲁棒且高效的物理启发性特征选择框架。

Method: 1) 构建样本间的稀疏相似图；2) 确定Nishimori温度β_N（Bethe Hessian矩阵奇异的临界温度）；3) 取最小特征向量捕获本征度校正扩散过程的主导模态，重加权节点避免枢纽主导；4) 转置数据矩阵后在特征空间应用NBSE，得到一维谱嵌入以揭示冗余/语义相关维度组；5) 通过平衡分箱每选一个代表特征。理论证明了彩色高斯扰动对β_N的偏移界为O(σ̄²)，保证鲁棒性。

Result: 在MobileNetV2和EfficientNet-B4的ImageNet嵌入上，NBSE在激进压缩下仍保持分类精度：EfficientNet-B4保留30%特征时精度下降<1%，优于ANOVA F检验和随机选择高达6.8%。

Conclusion: NBSE是一种无需贪心搜索、对噪声鲁棒的物理启发性特征选择方法，能在高压缩率下有效保留分类性能。

Abstract: We propose Noise-Based Spectral Embedding (NBSE), a physics-informed framework for selecting informative features from high-dimensional data without greedy search. NBSE constructs a sparse similarity graph on the samples and identifies the Nishimori temperature $β_N$ the critical inverse temperature at which the Bethe Hessian becomes singular. The corresponding smallest eigenvector captures the dominant mode of an intrinsically degree-corrected diffusion process, naturally reweighting nodes to prevent hub dominance. By transposing the data matrix and applying NBSE in feature space, we obtain a one-dimensional spectral embedding that reveals groups of redundant or semantically related dimensions; balanced binning then selects one representative per group. We prove that coloured Gaussian perturbations shift $β_N$ by at most $O(\barσ^2)$, guaranteeing robustness to measurement noise. Experiments on ImageNet embeddings from MobileNetV2 and EfficientNet-B4 show that NBSE preserves classification accuracy even under aggressive compression: on EfficientNet-B4 the accuracy drop is below $1\%$ when retaining only $30\%$ of features, outperforming ANOVA $F$-test and random selection by up to $6.8\%$.

</details>


### [344] [Scalable Hyperparameter-Divergent Ensemble Training with Automatic Learning Rate Exploration for Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24708)
*Hailing Cheng,Tao Huang,Chen Zhu,Antonio Alonso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HDET 利用数据并行训练中的多个 GPU 副本，通过交替的扇出（不同学习率独立训练）和收敛（参数平均）阶段，实现超参数探索与自动调度，无需额外训练预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据并行训练中 N 个 GPU 副本计算近乎相同的梯度更新，完全未探索学习率配置空间，造成计算资源浪费。

Method: 采用扇出-收敛交替阶段：扇出阶段各副本在结构化对称分布的学习率下独立训练；收敛阶段通过 AllReduce 平均参数。进一步提出 auto-LR 控制器，利用副本间相对损失作为性能信号，通过基于动量的无梯度元更新自动调整共享基础学习率调度。该框架可推广至任意标量超参数（如 dropout 率、注意力温度、权重衰减系数）。

Result: 生成自适应学习率调度，提升了优化质量和泛化能力，无需额外的超参数搜索或训练预算。

Conclusion: HDET 可作为 PyTorch OneCycleLR 调度器的即插即用替代方案，不改变模型架构、优化器或数据流水线，并能泛化至其他标量超参数的自动探索。

Abstract: Training large neural networks with data-parallel stochastic gradient descent allocates N GPU replicas to compute effectively identical updates -- a practice that leaves the rich space of learning rate configurations entirely unexplored during training. We propose Hyperparameter-Divergent Ensemble Training (HDET), a method that repurposes these replicas for simultaneous learning rate exploration at negligible communication overhead. HDET operates in alternating phases: a fan-out stage in which replicas train independently under a structured, symmetric spread of learning rates, and a converge stage in which parameters are averaged across all replicas via AllReduce every T steps. Building on this ensemble substrate, we further propose an automatic learning rate (auto-LR) controller that treats the relative training loss across replicas as a performance signal, updating the shared base schedule toward higher-performing configurations via a momentum-based gradient-free meta-update. The combined method produces a self-adapting learning rate schedule that improves both optimization quality and generalization without additional hyperparameter sweeps or training budget.
  Crucially, the framework generalizes beyond learning rate: any scalar hyperparameter that does not alter model architecture -- such as dropout rate, attention scale temperature, or weight-decay coefficient -- can be explored across replicas using the same fan-out/converge protocol, with inter-replica loss differences serving as zero-order hypergradients that guide the search direction. HDET is implemented as a drop-in replacement for PyTorch's OneCycleLR scheduler, requiring no changes to model architecture, optimizer, or data pipeline.

</details>


### [345] [SpecRLBench: A Benchmark for Generalization in Specification-Guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24729)
*Zijian Guo,İlker Işık,H. M. Sabbir Ahmad,Wenchao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SpecRLBench，一个用于评估基于LTL的规范引导强化学习方法泛化能力的基准，涵盖导航和操作领域中的多个难度级别，并在多种环境、机器人动力学和观测模式下进行实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规范引导强化学习方法在跨未见规范和多样化环境下的泛化能力尚未得到充分理解，缺乏系统性的评估基准。

Method: 构建SpecRLBench基准，涵盖导航和操作领域，包含静态和动态环境、多种机器人动力学和观测模式，设置多个难度级别，并对现有方法进行广泛的实证评估。

Result: 揭示了现有方法的优势与局限性，以及随着规范和环境复杂度增加所涌现的挑战。

Conclusion: SpecRLBench为系统比较和发展更具泛化能力的规范引导强化学习方法提供了结构化平台。

Abstract: Specification-guided reinforcement learning (RL) provides a principled framework for encoding complex, temporally extended tasks using formal specifications such as linear temporal logic (LTL). While recent methods have shown promising results, their ability to generalize across unseen specifications and diverse environments remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we introduce SpecRLBench, a benchmark designed to evaluate the generalization capabilities of LTL-based specification-guided RL methods. The benchmark spans multiple difficulty levels across navigation and manipulation domains, incorporating both static and dynamic environments, diverse robot dynamics, and varied observation modalities. Through extensive empirical evaluation, we characterize the strengths and limitations of existing approaches and reveal the challenges that emerge as specification and environment complexity increase. SpecRLBench provides a structured platform for systematic comparison and supports the development of more generalizable specification-guided RL methods. Code is available at https://github.com/BU-DEPEND-Lab/SpecRLBench.

</details>


### [346] [Learning to Think from Multiple Thinkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24737)
*Nirmit Joshi,Roey Magen,Nathan Srebro,Nikolaos Tsilivis,Gal Vardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多思考者链式思维（CoT）监督下的学习问题。在密码学假设下，被动收集多个思考者的CoT数据可能导致学习困难；但提出的主动学习算法能高效学习，所需每位思考者的CoT数据量与目标精度ε无关，思考者数量随log(1/ε)log log(1/ε)增长，并需足够的被动端结果数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在多个思考者提供不同但正确的CoT监督时，学习是否仍然高效，以及如何克服多源CoT监督带来的计算困难。

Method: 采用密码学假设下的理论分析，对比被动数据收集与主动学习两种设置；提出一种通用的计算高效主动学习算法。

Result: 被动设置下，来自两个或少数不同思考者的CoT监督可能导致学习困难；主动学习算法能以每位思考者少量CoT数据（与ε无关）、思考者数量O(log(1/ε)log log(1/ε))以及足够的被动端结果数据实现高效学习。

Conclusion: 多源CoT监督在被动设置下可能带来学习困难，但主动学习策略能有效克服这一挑战，实现样本高效的CoT学习。

Abstract: We study learning with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) supervision from multiple thinkers, all of whom provide correct but possibly systematically different solutions, e.g., step-by-step solutions to math problems written by different thinkers, or step-by-step execution traces of different programs solving the same problem.
  We consider classes that are computationally easy to learn using CoT supervision from a single thinker, but hard to learn with only end-result supervision, i.e., without CoT (Joshi et al. 2025). We establish that, under cryptographic assumptions, learning can be hard from CoT supervision provided by two or a few different thinkers, in passive data-collection settings.
  On the other hand, we provide a generic computationally efficient active learning algorithm that learns with a small amount of CoT data per thinker that is completely independent of the target accuracy $\varepsilon$, a moderate number of thinkers that scales as $\log \frac{1}{\varepsilon}\log \log \frac{1}{\varepsilon}$, and sufficient passive end-result data that scales as $\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\cdot poly\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}$.

</details>


### [347] [The Optimal Sample Complexity of Multiclass and List Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24749)
*Chirag Pabbaraju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了多类假设类的最大超图密度受其DS维数上界约束，解决了Daniely和Shalev-Shwartz（2014）提出的长期猜想，从而确定了多类分类和列表学习中样本复杂度对DS维数的最优依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多类分类的最优样本复杂度问题长期悬而未决，尽管已知相关复杂度参数为DS维数，但上下界之间存在√DS的差距。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 基于Hanneke等人（2026）对多类假设类DS维数的代数刻画，证明最大超图密度不超过DS维数，从而解决Daniely and Shalev-Shwartz（2014）的猜想。

Result: 成功证明了最大超图密度与DS维数之间的上界关系，消除了样本复杂度上下界之间的√DS差距，确定了最优依赖关系。

Conclusion: 本研究最终确定了多类分类和列表学习中样本复杂度对DS维数的最优依赖关系。

Abstract: While the optimal sample complexity of binary classification in terms of the VC dimension is well-established, determining the optimal sample complexity of multiclass classification has remained open. The appropriate complexity parameter for multiclass classification is the DS dimension, and despite significant efforts, a gap of $\sqrt{\text{DS}}$ has persisted between the upper and lower bounds on sample complexity.
  Recent work by Hanneke et al. (2026) shows a novel algebraic characterization of multiclass hypothesis classes in terms of their DS dimension. Building up on this, we show that the maximum hypergraph density of any multiclass hypothesis class is upper-bounded by its DS dimension. This proves a longstanding conjecture of Daniely and Shalev-Shwartz (2014). As a consequence, we determine the optimal dependence of the sample complexity on the DS dimension for multiclass as well as list learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [348] [Airspeed Forward-Invariance for Unpowered Fixed-Wing Aircraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22860)
*Huseyin Emre Tekaslan,Ella M. Atkins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于生存能力的空速保护方法，利用Nagumo切向条件推导出稳态风中地面参考制导的闭式风相关许可制导指令，保证空速安全包络的前向不变性，并通过离线二次规划框架认证非上升飞行的机动基元，在高保真无动力固定翼模型上验证了严格的空速有界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无动力固定翼飞行器在风影响下仅通过势能-动能交换调节空速，空速动态对制导指令高度敏感，现有方法缺乏对风影响下空速安全包络的严格保证。

Method: 利用Nagumo切向条件推导稳态风中空速安全包络前向不变性的闭式风相关许可制导指令特征，将其嵌入离线二次规划框架，认证非上升飞行的机动基元。

Result: 在高保真无动力固定翼模型上，通过拼接认证的机动基元形成滑翔轨迹，验证了严格的空速有界性。

Conclusion: 所提方法能在稳态风条件下有效保证空速安全，未来工作将拓展至非稳态风场和飞行实验。

Abstract: Autonomous fixed-wing flight is becoming a key capability in aerial robotics, enabling sensing, mobility, and contingency operations across both small-scale Uncrewed Aircraft Systems and large-scale Advanced Air Mobility. During unpowered operation in fixed-wing platforms, airspeed is regulated solely through potential-kinetic energy exchange, making airspeed dynamics highly sensitive to guidance commands, particularly under wind. This paper presents a viability-based airspeed protection for ground-referenced guidance in steady wind, where airspeed evolution depends explicitly on the commanded flight path angle. Leveraging Nagumo's tangency condition, we derive a closed-form, wind-dependent characterization of admissible guidance commands that guarantees forward invariance of a safe airspeed envelope. These conditions are embedded within an offline quadratic programming framework to certify airspeed-safe maneuver primitives for non-ascending flight at the guidance level. The approach is validated using a high-fidelity unpowered fixed-wing aircraft model on gliding trajectories formed by concatenating certified maneuver primitives, demonstrating strict airspeed boundedness. Future work will address unsteady wind fields and flight experiments.

</details>


### [349] [Magnetic Indoor Localization through CNN Regression and Rotation Invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22896)
*Helge Rosé,Konstantin Klipp,Tom Koubek,Bernd Schäufele,Ilja Radusch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用旋转不变特征（磁场模量Mn和重力轴投影Mg）替代原始3D磁数据训练轻量级扩张CNN（MagNetS/XL），在MagPie数据集上实现了对设备旋转鲁棒的室内定位，精度达到或超越当前最优水平，且小模型参数量仅为大模型的三分之一，适合移动端部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于CNN和磁场特征的室内定位方案中，原始3D磁力计数据对设备朝向高度敏感，导致模型在实际使用中因旋转而产生显著精度下降，需解决旋转鲁棒性问题以实现无需朝向对齐的可靠定位。

Method: 提取两种旋转不变特征——磁场模量（Mn）和重力轴投影（Mg）作为输入；设计轻量级7层扩张CNN架构（MagNetS/XL），直接回归（x,y）坐标；在MagPie数据集上系统评估固定和随机旋转对训练/测试数据的影响。

Result: 原始3D输入在90°固定旋转下各向同性误差增大，随机旋转下进一步恶化；而2D (Mn, Mg)输入在超出特定旋转阈值后精度超越3D输入（Loomis:0°, Talbot:5°, CSL:6°），且始终保持旋转不变精度。MagNetXL达到或超越SOTA，MagNetS以约1/3参数量实现相近性能。

Conclusion: 旋转不变特征带来的鲁棒性增益超过了输入维度降低带来的信息损失，在实际使用中可实现无需朝向对齐或额外基础设施的建图和定位，为移动端部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Indoor positioning is an essential technology for a wide range of applications in GNSS-denied environments, including indoor navigation and IoT systems. Combining convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and magnetic field-based features offers a low-cost, infrastructure-free solution for precise positioning. While magnetic fingerprints are a promising approach for indoor positioning, models trained on raw 3D magnetometer data are highly sensitive to device orientation. We address this by using two rotation invariant features derived from the 3D magnetic field: the norm (Mn) and the projection onto the gravity axis (Mg). We train a lightweight 7-layer dilated CNN (MagNetS/XL) on magnetic sequences to directly regress (x, y) positions. Using the MagPie dataset (three buildings, handheld trajectories), we systematically evaluate fixed and random rotations of test and/or train data. Raw 3D inputs (Mx, My , Mz) exhibit isotropic error increases under fixed 90° rotations and further degrade with growing random rotations. In contrast, 2D (Mn, Mg) inputs maintain rotation invariant accuracy and surpass the 3D inputs once rotation exceeds building-specific thresholds for three reference buildings: 0° for Loomis (large), 5° for Talbot (medium), and 6° for CSL (small). MagNetXL achieves or exceeds state-of-the-art accuracy on the MagPie dataset, and MagNetS delivers similar performance with roughly one third of the parameters, favoring mobile deployment. These results show that the robustness gained from rotation invariant inputs outweighs the loss of input dimensionality in realistic usage, allowing mapping and localization without orientation alignment or added infrastructure.

</details>


### [350] [RecoverFormer: End-to-End Contact-Aware Recovery for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22911)
*Zihui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RECOVERFORMER是一种端到端的人形机器人恢复策略，利用因果Transformer和两个新颖的头部结构（潜在恢复模式、接触可供性头部），在未见过环境和动力学失配下实现零样本泛化的多模态恢复行为，在Unitree G1平台上达到100%恢复成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在非结构化环境中运行时常受到意外扰动，而端到端控制策略在此场景下的恢复能力仍然具有挑战性，现有方法难以实现多模态、接触感知的鲁棒恢复。

Method: 提出RECOVERFORMER架构，包含：1）基于50步观测历史的因果Transformer；2）潜在恢复模式头部，实现不同恢复策略（补偿性迈步、手-环境接触、质心重塑）间的平滑切换；3）接触可供性头部，预测环境表面（墙壁、栏杆、桌边）是否有利于稳定。仅在开放地面训练，零样本迁移到有墙环境。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上：1）有墙环境100%恢复成功（100-300N推力，墙距0.25-1.4m）；2）零样本动力学失配下：+25%质量75.5%、30ms延迟89%、低摩擦91.5%、复合扰动99%。t-SNE分析验证潜在模式在无模式监督下按力区自动特化。

Conclusion: 单一端到端策略能够实现多模态、接触感知的人形机器人恢复，且对扰动幅度、接触几何和动力学变化具有强泛化能力。

Abstract: Humanoid robots operating in unstructured environments must recover from unexpected disturbances-a capability that remains challenging for end-to-end control policies. We present RECOVERFORMER, a fully end-to-end humanoid recovery policy that learns when and how to switch among recovery behaviors-including compensatory stepping, hand-environment contact, and center-of-mass reshaping-while maintaining robust performance under model mismatch. The architecture combines a causal transformer over a 50-step observation history with two novel heads: a latent recovery mode that enables smooth transitions among distinct recovery strategies, and a contact affordance head that predicts which environmental surfaces (walls, railings, table edges) are beneficial for stabilization. We evaluate RECOVERFORMER on the Unitree G1 humanoid in MuJoCo. Trained only on open floor, RECOVERFORMER transfers zero shot to walled environments, achieving 100% recovery success across 100-300 N pushes and across wall distances from 0.25-1.4m. Under zero-shot dynamics mismatch, RECOVERFORMER reaches 75.5% at plus +25% mass, 89% under 30 ms latency, 91.5% at low friction, and 99% under compound friction, latency and mass perturbation. The learned latent modes specialize across force regimes without mode-level supervision, validated by t-SNE analysis of 300 episodes. Taken together, these results show that a single end-to-end policy can deliver multi-modal, contact aware humanoid recovery that generalizes across perturbation magnitude, contact geometry, and dynamics shift.

</details>


### [351] [Learning from the Best: Smoothness-Driven Metrics for Data Quality in Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23000)
*Soham Kulkarni,Raayan Dhar,Yuchen Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RINSE框架，利用轨迹平滑度（SAL和TED两个互补指标）对演示数据进行质量评分，无需策略训练或人工标注即可筛选高质量数据，在多个基准测试中显著提升BC性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准行为克隆(BC)平等对待所有演示数据，但现实数据集因操作者技能差异、遥操作伪影和程序不一致性而包含不同质量的轨迹，现有数据筛选方法需要昂贵的策略训练循环或人工标注，可扩展性受限。

Method: 提出RINSE框架，基于运动控制理论中"平滑度是熟练运动的标志"这一原理，使用两个互补指标：（1）谱弧长(SAL)——频域正则性的谱度量；（2）轨迹包络距离(TED)——接触感知几何偏差的空间度量。SAL和TED均无需策略架构信息，仅基于轨迹数据即可计算。

Result: 在RoboMimic上，SAL筛选仅用1/6数据实现16%成功率提升；在真实操作任务上，TED筛选用一半数据实现20%提升；在LIBERO-10的STRAP检索过滤中，RINSE重排序提升5.6%平均成功率；在Re-Mix领域重加权中，RINSE得分与Re-Mix分配高度相关(Spearman ρ≥0.89)。

Conclusion: 平滑度是一种有用的数据质量信号，可在过滤、检索和重加权等多种设置中有效提升BC性能，尤其在数据噪声大或异构的场景下效果显著。

Abstract: In behavioral cloning (BC), policy performance is fundamentally limited by demonstration data quality. Real-world datasets contain trajectories of varying quality due to operator skill differences, teleoperation artifacts, and procedural inconsistencies, yet standard BC treats all demonstrations equally. Existing curation methods require costly policy training in the loop or manual annotation, limiting scalability. We propose RINSE (Ranking and INdexing Smooth Examples), a lightweight framework for scoring demonstrations based on trajectory smoothness that is policy-architecture-agnostic and operates on trajectory data alone, with TED additionally using a phase-boundary/contact signal. Grounded in motor control theory, which establishes smoothness as a hallmark of skilled movement, RINSE uses two complementary metrics: Spectral Arc Length (SAL), a spectral measure of frequency-domain regularity, and Trajectory-Envelope Distance (TED), a spatial measure of contact-aware geometric deviation. We show that smoothness filtering can reduce the conditional action variance of the retained data distribution, with downstream effects that can be amplified by action chunking and compounding error. On RoboMimic benchmarks, SAL filtering achieves 16% higher success using one-sixth of the data. On real-world manipulation, TED filtering achieves 20% improvement with half the data. As a retrieval-stage filter within STRAP on LIBERO-10, RINSE re-ranking improves mean success by 5.6%. As soft weights in Re-Mix domain reweighting, RINSE scores produce domain allocations highly correlated with the learned Re-Mix allocations (Spearman $ρ\geq 0.89$). These results support smoothness as a useful quality signal across filtering, retrieval, and reweighting settings, especially in noisy or heterogeneous data regimes.

</details>


### [352] [Vision-Language-Action in Robotics: A Survey of Datasets, Benchmarks, and Data Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23001)
*Ziyao Wang,Bingying Wang,Hanrong Zhang,Tingting Du,Tianyang Chen,Guoheng Sun,Yexiao He,Zheyu Shen,Wanghao Ye,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型进行了以数据为中心的系统性分析，围绕数据集、基准评测和数据引擎三大支柱展开，指出数据基础设施而非模型架构将成为VLA未来发展的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLA模型取得了显著进展，但底层数据基础设施这一核心瓶颈尚未得到充分审视。本文旨在系统性地揭示数据集、基准评测和数据引擎中存在的根本性缺陷，并主张将数据基础设施作为一等研究问题来对待。

Method: 采用以数据为中心的分析视角，将VLA研究组织为三个支柱进行系统性梳理：(1) 对真实世界与合成数据集从具身多样性、模态组成和动作空间公式三个维度进行分类；(2) 联合分析基准评测的任务复杂性与环境结构；(3) 审视基于仿真、视频重建和自动任务生成的三种数据引擎范式。

Result: 揭示了数据集的保真度-成本权衡、基准评测在组合泛化和长程推理评估上的结构性缺失、以及数据引擎在物理真实性和仿真到现实迁移上的共同局限性。最终归纳出表示对齐、多模态监督、推理评估和可扩展数据生成四个开放挑战。

Conclusion: 未来VLA的进步将更依赖于高保真数据引擎与结构化评估协议的协同设计，而非模型架构本身。数据基础设施应被提升为一等研究问题，而非背景性关注点。

Abstract: Despite remarkable progress in Vision--Language--Action (VLA) models, a central bottleneck remains underexamined: the data infrastructure that underlies embodied learning. In this survey, we argue that future advances in VLA will depend less on model architecture and more on the co-design of high-fidelity data engines and structured evaluation protocols. To this end, we present a systematic, data-centric analysis of VLA research organized around three pillars: datasets, benchmarks, and data engines. For datasets, we categorize real-world and synthetic corpora along embodiment diversity, modality composition, and action space formulation, revealing a persistent fidelity-cost trade-off that fundamentally constrains large-scale collection. For benchmarks, we analyze task complexity and environment structure jointly, exposing structural gaps in compositional generalization and long-horizon reasoning evaluation that existing protocols fail to address. For data engines, we examine simulation-based, video-reconstruction, and automated task-generation paradigms, identifying their shared limitations in physical grounding and sim-to-real transfer. Synthesizing these analyses, we distill four open challenges: representation alignment, multimodal supervision, reasoning assessment, and scalable data generation. Addressing them, we argue, requires treating data infrastructure as a first-class research problem rather than a background concern.

</details>


### [353] [Equivariant Filter for Radar-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23033)
*Giulio Delama,Jan Michalczyk,Morten Nissov,Martin Scheiber,Alessandro Fornasier,Kostas Alexis,Stephan Weiss*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于等变滤波器（EqF）的雷达-惯性里程计（RIO）方法，利用李群对称性将导航状态与IMU偏置几何耦合，并扩展至雷达-IMU外参标定与多状态约束更新。该方法在保持一致性和鲁棒性方面优于传统EKF-RIO，尤其在外参初始化错误时仍能收敛，在两个无人机平台上验证了其先进精度和强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）的雷达-惯性里程计（RIO）严重依赖精确的雷达-IMU外参标定，且对大线性化误差敏感，容易性能退化甚至发散。

Method: 提出等变滤波器（EqF），基于李群对称性几何耦合导航状态与IMU偏置，并扩展至包含雷达-IMU外参标定和多状态约束更新，利用等变性质保持一致性并增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在两个不同无人机平台上的实物实验表明，EqF-RIO在外参正确标定时达到最先进精度，在外参存在大误差时仍能收敛，而传统EKF-RIO则失效。

Conclusion: 等变滤波器框架有效提升了雷达-惯性里程计在标定误差下的鲁棒性和一致性，为实际部署提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Radar-Inertial Odometry (RIO) based on the Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) relies on accurate extrinsic calibration between the radar and the Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) and is sensitive to disturbances, as large linearization errors can degrade performance or even cause divergence. To address these limitations, this letter proposes an Equivariant Filter (EqF) for RIO based on a Lie group symmetry that geometrically couples navigation states and IMU biases, extending it to incorporate radar-IMU extrinsic calibration and multi-state constraint updates. This equivariant formulation inherently preserves consistency and enhances robustness, enabling reliable state estimation even under poor or completely wrong initialization of calibration states. Real-world experiments on two different Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) show that the proposed EqF-RIO achieves state-of-the-art accuracy under correct extrinsic calibration and offers improved convergence under large calibration errors, where the conventional EKF-RIO fails. Evaluation code is open-sourced.

</details>


### [354] [Control Barrier Functions Solved with Hierarchical Quadratic Programming for Safe Physical Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23039)
*Rui Luo,Jonas Mariager Jakobsen,Wesley Roozing,Federico Califano,Cheng Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于控制障碍函数（CBF）的分层二次规划（HQP）框架，用于物理人机交互中灵活平衡安全任务与性能任务，并在真实冗余机器人上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理人机交互中安全至关重要，现有CBF方法在任务冲突时难以保证可行性，需要一种更灵活的框架来分层管理安全与性能任务。

Method: 采用CBF-based Hierarchical Quadratic Programming (HQP)框架，将性能任务（如期望的人机交互行为）和安全任务按层次结构设计，通过分层松弛处理冲突任务，确保求解可行性。

Result: 在真实冗余机器人上进行了大量实验，验证了该方法在有效性、灵活性和通用性方面的优势。

Conclusion: CBF-HQP框架能够更灵活地平衡物理人机交互中的安全与性能需求，为多任务冲突场景提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Physical human-robot interaction offers the potential to leverage human intelligence and robot physical capabilities to enable a range of exciting applications, e.g., collaborative robots for rehabilitation. Safety is critical for the successful deployment of this kind of robotic system. In recent years, Control Barrier Function (CBF) has emerged as an effective approach to enforce safety guarantees, which has been widely applied in various applications, from adaptive cruise control to navigation of legged robots. CBFs can be solved in a Quadratic Programming (QP) problem, which can include many CBF-formulated tasks. To manage a large number of safety tasks, a hierarchical CBF has been used to allow hierarchical relaxation of safety tasks to ensure the feasibility of a solution in the presence of conflicting tasks. In this work, we propose to use a CBF-based Hierarchical Quadratic Programming (HQP) framework in physical human-robot interaction to allow us to design both performance tasks (e.g., preserve the desired behavior at the human-robot interaction point) and safety tasks at any level of a hierarchy to balance the safety and the performance in a more flexible way. Extensive experiments were carried out on a real redundant robot to validate the effectiveness, flexibility, and generality of this approach.

</details>


### [355] [A Lightweight Toggleable Adhesion Prototype for Multirotor UAV Landing on Tilting Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23074)
*Teighin Nordholt,Melissa Greeff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级可切换粘附机制，通过马达驱动的螺旋钻与着陆面钩环材料配合，使多旋翼飞行器能在倾斜达43度的无人水面舰艇上可靠着陆，成功率从40%提升至80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主多旋翼飞行器在无人水面舰艇上着陆面临波浪引起的倾斜、风扰动和着陆区域有限的挑战，现有方法位姿容忍度小，难以保证可靠着陆。

Method: 设计了一种轻量级可切换粘附机构，采用马达驱动的螺旋钻与着陆表面钩环材料（魔术贴）啮合，实现着陆时主动粘附与起飞时受控脱离；使用改进的Crazyflie 2.0和固定角度倾斜平台进行验证，仅采用简单垂直PID控制器。

Result: 在平台倾斜高达43度条件下，采用适当驱动设置，着陆成功率从基线40%提升至80%。

Conclusion: 所提出的可切换粘附机制能够显著提升多旋翼飞行器在倾斜表面上的着陆可靠性，为极端海况下的自主作业提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous multirotor landings on uncrewed surface vessels (USVs) are critical for persistent maritime operations but remain challenging due to wave-induced tilt, wind disturbances, and limited landing area. Many existing approaches exhibit small pose tolerance for reliable landing. This paper presents a lightweight toggleable adhesion mechanism to improve landing reliability. The system uses a motor-driven corkscrew that engages hook-and-loop material on the landing surface, enabling active adhesion during landing and controlled release during takeoff. We evaluate a prototype using a modified Crazyflie 2.0 and a custom tilting platform at fixed angles representative of extreme wave conditions. Using only a simple vertical PID controller, the proposed approach increases landing success from an average of 40% (baseline) to 80% across platform tilts up to 43 degrees using appropriately selected actuation settings.

</details>


### [356] [Breaking Lock-In: Preserving Steerability under Low-Data VLA Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23121)
*Suning Huang,Jiaqi Shao,Ke Wang,Qianzhong Chen,Jiankai Sun,Yanjiang Guo,Mac Schwager,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeLock通过保留视觉定位和测试时对比提示引导，解决VLA策略在少量数据微调后出现的概念锁定和空间锁定问题，无需额外监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略在少量演示数据集上进行后训练（SFT）后，会出现锁定现象：对新指令不再响应，仅限于训练时见过的行为。具体表现为概念锁定（固着于训练物体/属性）和空间锁定（固着于训练空间目标）。现有方法需要额外监督信号或增强数据集。

Method: DeLock包含两个核心组件：(1) 在后训练过程中保留视觉定位能力；(2) 测试时使用对比提示引导（contrastive prompt guidance）来根据新指令调控策略的去噪动力学。

Result: 在8个模拟和真实世界评估中，DeLock持续优于强基线方法，其性能匹配或超过使用大量精选演示数据后训练的最先进通用策略。

Conclusion: 策略内部预训练知识足以解决锁定问题，无需额外监督信号或增强数据。DeLock有效缓解了后训练导致的泛化能力丧失。

Abstract: Have you ever post-trained a generalist vision-language-action (VLA) policy on a small demonstration dataset, only to find that it stops responding to new instructions and is limited to behaviors observed during post-training? We identify this phenomenon as lock-in: after low-data, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), the policy becomes overly specialized to the post-training data and fails to generalize to novel instructions, manifesting as concept lock-in (fixation on training objects/attributes) and spatial lock-in (fixation on training spatial targets). Many existing remedies introduce additional supervision signals, such as those derived from foundation models or auxiliary objectives, or rely on augmented datasets to recover generalization. In this paper, we show that the policy's internal pre-trained knowledge is sufficient: DeLock mitigates lock-in by preserving visual grounding during post-training and applying test-time contrastive prompt guidance to steer the policy's denoising dynamics according to novel instructions. Across eight simulation and real-world evaluations, DeLock consistently outperforms strong baselines and matches or exceeds the performance of a state-of-the-art generalist policy post-trained with substantially more curated demonstrations.

</details>


### [357] [BridgeACT: Bridging Human Demonstrations to Robot Actions via Unified Tool-Target Affordances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23249)
*Yifan Han,Jianxiang Liu,Haoyu Zhang,Yuqi Gu,Yunhan Guo,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BridgeACT提出了一种仅从人类视频学习机器人操作技能的无监督框架，通过将可操作性作为跨具身的中间表征（包括抓取位置预测和3D运动可操作性），无需任何机器人演示数据即可实现真实世界部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用人类视频的规模性和多样性来学习机器人操作具有吸引力，但将人类演示迁移到可执行的机器人行为仍面临挑战。现有方法要么依赖机器人数据进行下游适配，要么学习的可供性表征停留在感知层面，无法直接支持真实世界执行。

Method: BridgeACT将操作分解为"抓取位置"和"运动方式"两个互补问题：(1) 通过任务相关的可供性区域定位当前场景中的抓取点；(2) 从人类演示中预测任务条件化的3D运动可供性。这些可供性通过抓取模块和轻量级闭环运动控制器映射为机器人动作。复杂任务被表示为可供性操作的组合。

Result: 在真实世界操作任务中，BridgeACT优于先前基线方法，并且能够泛化到未见过的物体、场景和视角。

Conclusion: 通过将可供性作为具身无关的中间表征，仅从人类视频即可学习可部署的机器人操作技能，无需任何机器人演示数据。

Abstract: Learning robot manipulation from human videos is appealing due to the scale and diversity of human demonstrations, but transferring such demonstrations to executable robot behavior remains challenging. Prior work either relies on robot data for downstream adaptation or learns affordance representations that remain at the perception level and do not directly support real-world execution. We present BridgeACT, an affordance-driven framework that learns robotic manipulation directly from human videos without requiring any robot demonstration data. Our key idea is to model affordance as an embodiment-agnostic intermediate representation that bridges human demonstrations and robot actions. BridgeACT decomposes manipulation into two complementary problems: where to grasp and how to move. To this end, BridgeACT first grounds task-relevant affordance regions in the current scene, and then predicts task-conditioned 3D motion affordances from human demonstrations. The resulting affordances are mapped to robot actions through a grasping module and a lightweight closed-loop motion controller, enabling direct deployment on real robots. In addition, we represent complex manipulation tasks as compositions of affordance operations, which allows a unified treatment of diverse tasks and object-to-object interactions. Experiments on real-world manipulation tasks show that BridgeACT outperforms prior baselines and generalizes to unseen objects, scenes, and viewpoints.

</details>


### [358] [Modular Sensory Stream for Integrating Physical Feedback in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23272)
*Jimin Lee,Huiwon Jang,Myungkyu Koo,Jungwoo Park,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoSS提出模块化感知流框架，通过解耦模态流、两阶段训练和辅助预测任务，使视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）能有效融合多种物理信号（如触觉和力矩），在真实实验中实现了多模态协同的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型仅依赖视觉感知，而真实世界交互需要多种物理反馈信号；已有工作仅关注单一物理信号，未能利用多种异构物理信号的互补性。

Method: 提出MoSS框架：(1) 解耦模态流，通过联合跨模态自注意力将多种物理信号集成到动作流中；(2) 两阶段训练策略，早期冻结预训练VLA参数以保证稳定融合；(3) 辅助任务预测未来物理信号，以增强对接触交互动态的建模能力。

Result: 真实世界实验表明，MoSS成功增强了VLA模型利用多种物理信号（触觉、力矩）的能力，且多种信号融合能产生协同的性能增益。

Conclusion: MoSS有效解决了VLA模型融合多模态物理信号的问题，模块化设计使其可灵活扩展不同物理感知模态，为机器人操作中的多模态感知提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Humans understand and interact with the real world by relying on diverse physical feedback beyond visual perception. Motivated by this, recent approaches attempt to incorporate physical sensory signals into Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs). However, they typically focus on a single type of physical signal, failing to capture the heterogeneous and complementary nature of real-world interactions. In this paper, we propose MoSS, a modular sensory stream framework that adapts VLAs to leverage multiple sensory signals for action prediction. Specifically, we introduce decoupled modality streams that integrate heterogeneous physical signals into the action stream via joint cross-modal self-attention. To enable stable incorporation of new modalities, we adopt a two-stage training scheme that freezes pretrained VLA parameters in the early stage. Furthermore, to better capture contact interaction dynamics, we incorporate an auxiliary task that predicts future physical signals. Through extensive real-world experiments, we demonstrate that MoSS successfully augments VLAs to leverage diverse physical signals (i.e., tactile and torque), integrating multiple signals to achieve synergistic performance gains.

</details>


### [359] [An Efficient Beam Search Algorithm for Active Perception in Mobile Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23327)
*Kaixian Qu,Han Wang,Victor Klemm,Cesar Cadena,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出节点束搜索算法和快速探索随机环图，结合期望增益指标，在主动感知任务中显著优于现有方法，并已在真实机器人平台上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动感知方法要么求解计算昂贵的旅行商问题，要么采用过于约束的最短路径树，缺乏可扩展且高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出节点束搜索算法（每个节点保留前B个候选路径）、期望增益指标（融合前沿探索思想）以及快速探索随机环图（保持全方向采样并通过回退局部规划器确保连通性）。

Result: 在三个代表性主动感知任务中，NBS结合RRAG均取得最高性能，在至少一个任务上超越最先进算法20%以上，并在真实机器人平台上得到验证。

Conclusion: 节点束搜索与快速探索随机环图相结合，在保持可扩展性的同时显著提升了主动感知任务的性能。

Abstract: Active perception is a fundamental problem in autonomous robotics in which the robot must decide where to move and what to sense in order to obtain the most informative observations for accomplishing its mission. Existing approaches either solve a computationally expensive traveling salesman problem over heuristically selected informative nodes, or adopt a more efficient but overly constrained shortest path tree formulation. To address these limitations, we explore beam search algorithms as scalable alternatives. While the standard beam search provides scalability by preserving the top-B paths at each depth level, it is prone to local optima and exhibits parameter sensitivity. Our first contribution is a node-wise beam search (NBS) algorithm, which maintains top-B candidates per node to enable more effective exploration of the solution space. Systematic benchmarking on graphs shows that NBS consistently outperforms other baselines and maintains strong performance even at low beam widths. As a second contribution, we integrate the concept of frontiers into the path selection criterion, introducing the expected gain metric, which better balances exploration and exploitation compared to existing alternatives. Our third contribution proposes the rapidly-exploring random annulus graph (RRAG), a novel graph construction method that preserves full orientation sampling and ensures connectivity in cluttered environments through a fallback local sampling-based planner. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NBS combined with RRAG achieves the highest performance across all three representative active perception tasks, outperforming state-of-the-art algorithms by at least 20% in one or more tasks. We further validate the approach on real robotic platforms in different scenarios.

</details>


### [360] [Learning from Demonstration with Failure Awareness for Safe Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23360)
*Xianghui Wang,Siwei Cheng,Shanze Wang,Xinming Zhang,Dan Zhang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种解耦成功和失败数据的故障感知学习框架，通过将失败数据用于价值估计而非策略学习，在保持任务成功率的同时显著降低机器人的碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从演示学习中，演示数据主要由成功行为组成，对不安全状态覆盖有限，导致机器人在遇到分布外场景时安全性差。碰撞等失败经验包含不安全区域的关键信息，但未被有效利用，因为失败数据不能为动作模仿提供有效指导，简单纳入策略学习反而会降低性能。

Method: 提出一个故障感知学习框架，显式解耦成功和失败数据的角色：失败数据用于塑造危险区域的价值估计（通过离线强化学习），而策略学习仅限制在成功演示上，使失败数据得到有效利用而不破坏策略行为。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的实验表明，该框架能够持续降低碰撞率，同时保持任务成功率，并在不同环境和机器人平台上展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过解耦成功与失败数据在策略学习中的角色，可以安全有效地利用失败经验来提升机器人导航的安全性，而不会对策略性能产生负面影响。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration is widely used for robot navigation, yet it suffers from a fundamental limitation: demonstrations consist predominantly of successful behaviors and provide limited coverage of unsafe states. This limitation leads to poor safety when the robot encounters scenarios beyond the demonstration distribution. Failure experiences, such as collisions, contain essential information about unsafe regions, but remain underutilized. The key difficulty lies in the fact that failure data do not provide valid guidance for action imitation, and their naive incorporation into policy learning often degrades performance. We address this challenge by proposing a failure-aware learning framework that explicitly decouples the roles of success and failure data. In this framework, failure experiences are used to shape value estimation in hazardous regions, while policy learning is restricted to successful demonstrations. This separation enables the effective use of failure data without corrupting policy behavior. We implement this design within an offline reinforcement learning (RL) setting and evaluate it in both simulation and real-world environments. The results show that our framework consistently reduces collision rates while preserving the task success rate, and demonstrate strong generalization across different environments and robot platforms.

</details>


### [361] [EgoLive: A Large-Scale Egocentric Dataset from Real-World Human Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23570)
*Yihang Li,Xuelong Wei,Jingzhou Luo,Yingjing Xiao,Yibo Bai,Guangyuan Zhou,Teng Zou,Chenguang Gui,Jiajun Wen,He Zhang,Kangliang Chen,Xing Pan,Shuaiyan Liu,Daming Wang,Tao An,Jiayi Li,Shibo Jin,Wanwan Zhang,Tianyu Wang,Boren Wei,Zhixuan Huang,Fangsheng Liu,Ruodai Li,Hui Zhang,Anson Li,Yicheng Gong,Peng Cao,Jiaming Liang,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoLive是一个大规模、高质量的第一人称视角数据集，专门为机器人操作学习设计。它通过自研头戴式采集设备在无约束的真实场景中采集数据，涵盖家庭服务、零售等垂直工作场景，具备最大规模开源标注、领先数据质量和生态效度高等三大优势，旨在推动通用机器人模型的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人学习受限于大规模高质量数据集的稀缺，现有数据采集方法（遥操作、通用操作界面）在可扩展性和真实部署方面存在固有局限，而人类第一人称视频采集提供了一种可扩展、自然且无约束的数据收集途径。

Method: 采用定制头戴式采集设备，在无约束的真实世界场景中收集第一人称视频数据，涵盖家庭服务、零售等垂直工作场景，并进行高精度多模态标注，构建大规模开源注释数据集。

Result: EgoLive是目前最大的专注于真实世界任务导向的人类日常活动的开源标注第一人称数据集，具备领先的数据质量（通过定制设备和高精度多模态标注实现），以及在无约束场景下包含垂直领域工作数据所带来的优越多样性和生态效度。

Conclusion: EgoLive为研究社区提供了一个可扩展、高质量的数据集，有望加速通用机器人模型的突破性进展，并促进机器人系统在现实世界中的部署。

Abstract: The advancement of robot learning is currently hindered by the scarcity of large-scale, high-quality datasets. While established data collection methods such as teleoperation and universal manipulation interfaces dominate current datasets, they suffer from inherent limitations in scalability and real-world deployability. Human egocentric video collection, by contrast, has emerged as a promising approach to enable scalable, natural and in-the-wild data collection. As such, we present EgoLive, a large-scale, high-quality egocentric dataset designed explicitly for robot manipulation learning. EgoLive establishes three distinctive technical advantages over existing egocentric datasets: first, it represents the largest open-source annotated egocentric dataset focused on real-world task-oriented human routines to date; second, it delivers leading data quality via a customized head-mounted capture device and comprehensive high-precision multi-modal annotations; third, all data is collected exclusively in unconstrained real-world scenarios and encompasses vertical field human working data, including home service, retail, and other practical work scenarios, providing superior diversity and ecological validity. With the introduction of EgoLive, we aim to provide the research community with a scalable, high-quality dataset that accelerates breakthroughs in generalizable robotic models and facilitates the real-world deployment of robot systems.

</details>


### [362] [Tube Diffusion Policy: Reactive Visual-Tactile Policy Learning for Contact-rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23609)
*Teng Xue,Alberto Rigo,Bingjian Huang,Jiayi Shen,Zhengtong Xu,Nick Colonnese,Amirhossein H. Memar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Tube Diffusion Policy (TDP)，一种结合扩散模型与管状反馈控制的视觉-触觉模仿学习框架，通过动作管（action tube）实现接触丰富场景下的快速反应控制，在多项基准和真实实验中优于现有方法，并能显著减少去噪步骤以支持实时高频反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于动作分块的模仿学习方法在执行过程中无法对意外观察做出反应，尤其在接触丰富任务中，物理不确定性和高频触觉反馈要求快速反应控制，现有方法存在根本局限。

Method: 提出Tube Diffusion Policy (TDP)，将扩散模型与管状反馈控制相结合，学习以观察为条件的反馈流包裹名义动作分块，形成动作管（action tube），实现执行过程中的快速自适应反应。

Result: 在Push-T基准和三个视觉-触觉灵巧操作任务上，TDP一致优于最先进的模仿学习基线；两个真实世界实验验证了其在接触不确定性和外部干扰下的鲁棒反应能力；动作管的逐步校正机制显著减少了所需去噪步骤。

Conclusion: TDP通过结合生成模型的表达能力和管状反馈控制，有效解决了接触丰富操作中反应性不足的问题，同时适合实时高频反馈控制场景。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation is central to many everyday human activities, requiring continuous adaptation to contact uncertainty and external disturbances through multi-modal perception, particularly vision and tactile feedback. While imitation learning has shown strong potential for learning complex manipulation behaviors, most existing approaches rely on action chunking, which fundamentally limits their ability to react to unforeseen observations during execution. This limitation becomes especially critical in contact-rich scenarios, where physical uncertainty and high-frequency tactile feedback demand rapid, reactive control. To address this challenge, we propose Tube Diffusion Policy (TDP), a novel reactive visual-tactile policy learning framework that bridges diffusion-based imitation learning with tube-based feedback control. By leveraging the expressive power of generative models, TDP learns an observation-conditioned feedback flow around nominal action chunks, forming an action tube that enables fast and adaptive reactions during execution. We evaluate TDP on the widely used Push-T benchmark and three additional challenging visual-tactile dexterous manipulation tasks. Across all benchmarks, TDP consistently outperforms state-of-the-art imitation learning baselines. Two real-world experiments further validate its robust reactivity under contact uncertainty and external disturbances. Moreover, the step-wise correction mechanism enabled by action tube significantly reduces the required denoising steps, making TDP well suited for real-time, high-frequency feedback control in contact-rich manipulation.

</details>


### [363] [Move-Then-Operate: Behavioral Phasing for Human-Like Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23620)
*Haoming Xu,Lei Lei,Jie Gu,Chu Tang,Jingmin Chen,Ruiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Move-Then-Operate框架，将机器人操作显式解耦为粗放移动和接触关键操作两个行为阶段，通过可学习的相位选择器路由双专家策略，在RoboTwin2基准上取得68.9%平均成功率，比单体π0基线提升24%，匹配或超越使用10倍数据训练的模型，且训练步骤减少40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单体策略将移动和操作两种异质行为混为一谈，缺乏对相位特定动力学的结构先验，导致高精度操作任务表现不佳。

Method: 提出Move-Then-Operate框架：(1) 显式解耦为粗放移动和接触关键操作两个阶段；(2) 采用双专家策略架构，由可学习的相位选择器路由；(3) 通过基于MLLM的流水线自动生成相位标签，结合末端执行器速度和子任务分解等上下文线索。

Result: RoboTwin2基准上成功率68.9%，比单体π0基线提升24%；匹配或超越10倍数据量训练的模型；训练步骤减少40%达到峰值性能。

Conclusion: 移动与操作阶段的结构化解耦是高精度操作任务中高效且有效的策略，说明架构层面的行为分离比单纯扩大数据规模更具优势。

Abstract: We present Move-Then-Operate, a Vision language action framework that explicitly decouples robotic manipulation into two distinct behavioral phases: coarse relocation (move) and contact-critical interaction (operate). Unlike monolithic policies that conflate these heterogeneous regimes, our architecture employs a dual-expert policy routed by a learnable phase selector, introducing a structural inductive bias that isolates phase-specific dynamics. Phase labels are automatically generated via an MLLM-based pipeline conditioned on lightweight contextual cues such as end-effector velocity and subtask decomposition to ensure alignment with human motor patterns. Evaluated on the RoboTwin2 benchmark, our method achieves an average success rate of $68.9\%$, outperforming the monolithic $π_0$ baseline by $24\%$. It matches or exceeds models trained on $10\times$ more data and reaches peak performance in $40\%$ fewer training steps, demonstrating that architectural disentanglement of move and operate phases is a highly effective and efficient strategy for mastering high-precision manipulation.

</details>
