### [364] [Safe Navigation in Unknown and Cluttered Environments via Direction-Aware Convex Free-Region Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23648)
*Zhicheng Song,Yongjian Li,Kai Chen,Yulin Li,Fan Shi,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种将候选运动方向和机器人几何形状联合融入凸自由区域生成的导航框架，通过连续安全轨迹生成实现无碰撞运动，并在2D/3D及真实机器人上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在杂乱环境中扩大局部碰撞-free区域时，主要依据周围障碍物几何形状，未能同时兼顾机器人几何形状和候选运动方向的可通行延伸，导致下游运动受限，尤其在狭窄通道中。此外，离散化轨迹采样的安全检查无法保证机器人几何形状明确建模时的连续无碰撞运动。

Method: 提出FRGraph框架：(1) 将候选运动方向和机器人几何形状联合纳入凸自由区域生成；(2) 在每个区域内进行几何感知的目标位姿选择和轨迹生成；(3) 基于Lipschitz条件的连续安全认证与局部细化；(4) 基于区域的图结构维护自由区域和候选运动，支持增量规划。

Result: 在杂乱2D导航场景中的定量结果表明，所提方法生成的自由区域更有利于下游运动，实现可靠的无碰撞导航。3D和真实世界实验（四足机器人和无人机）验证了框架的可扩展性和实际应用性。

Conclusion: 将机器人几何和运动方向显式融入凸区域生成，并结合连续安全认证，能够有效提升杂乱环境下的导航可靠性和通过性。

Abstract: Convex free regions provide a structured and optimization-friendly representation of collision-free space for robot navigation in unknown and cluttered environments. However, existing methods typically enlarge local collision-free regions mainly according to surrounding obstacle geometry. In cluttered environments, such strategies may fail to generate regions that both accommodate robot geometry and preserve traversable extension along candidate motion directions, thereby limiting downstream traversal, especially in narrow passages. Even when such a region is available, safe motion generation remains challenging, because safety checking at discretized trajectory samples does not guarantee continuously collision-free motion when robot geometry is modeled explicitly. To address these issues, we propose a navigation framework that jointly incorporates candidate motion directions and robot geometry into convex free-region generation, and achieves continuously collision-free motion through continuous-safe trajectory generation. Within each region, the framework performs geometry-aware target pose selection and trajectory generation, together with Lipschitz-based continuous safety certification and local refinement. The resulting free regions and candidate motions are maintained in a region-based graph to support incremental planning. Quantitative results in cluttered 2D navigation scenarios show that the proposed method generates free regions better aligned with downstream traversal and enables reliable collision-free navigation, while additional 3D and real-world experiments on a quadrupedal robot and a UAV demonstrate the extensibility and practical applicability of the framework. The open-source project can be found at https://github.com/ZhichengSong6/FRGraph.

</details>


### [365] [Decentralized Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Collaborative Exploration for Indoor and Outdoor 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23693)
*Yuxiang Li,Kun Chen,Jiancheng Wang,Shihao Fang,Haoyao Chen,Yunhui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向异构多机器人系统的去中心化协作框架，通过改进的超体素分割、能力-任务匹配建模、HMDMTSP优化分配及冲突消解策略，实现室内外3D环境的高效自主探索，在探索效率和通信开销上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构多机器人系统在复杂环境中具有显著适应性，但如何充分发挥机器人之间的协作潜力仍是核心挑战。现有方法难以有效建模异构机器人的差异化能力与任务需求之间的匹配关系，导致探索效率受限。

Method: 1) 设计融合地形与观测指标的基础感知地图，采用改进超体素分割简化地图结构并支持轻量通信；2) 建模异构机器人的遍历与观测能力以评估任务视图需求；3) 将视图-簇分配建模为异构多仓库多旅行商问题(HMDMTSP)，并设计改进遗传算法求解；4) 消去冗余视图以优化探索路径，并解决机器人运动路径冲突。

Result: 在杂乱室内外环境中的仿真与实地实验表明，该方法能有效协调异构机器人间的探索任务，在探索效率和通信节省方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提去中心化协作框架通过精细的能力-任务匹配与全局优化分配，充分释放了异构机器人的协同潜力，为复杂3D环境下的多机器人自主探索提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Heterogeneous multi-robot systems feature significant adaptability for complex environments. However, effective collaboration that fully exploits the robots' potential remains a core challenge. This paper proposes a decentralized collaborative framework for heterogeneous multi-robot systems to autonomously explore indoor and outdoor 3D environments. First, a basic perception map that integrates terrain and observation metrics is designed. Improved supervoxel segmentation is developed to simplify the map structure and form a high-level representation that supports lightweight communication. Second, the traversal and observation capabilities of heterogeneous robots are modeled to evaluate the requirements of task views derived from incomplete supervoxels. These task views are grouped by requirements and clustered to streamline assignment. Subsequently, the view-cluster assignment is formulated as a heterogeneous multi-depot multi-traveling salesman problem (HMDMTSP) that incorporates constraints between view-cluster requirements and robot capabilities. An improved genetic algorithm is developed to efficiently solve this problem while ensuring global consistency. Based on the assignments, redundant views within clusters are eliminated to refine exploration routes. Finally, conflicts between robots' motion paths are resolved. Simulations and field experiments in cluttered indoor and outdoor environments demonstrate that our approach effectively coordinates exploration tasks among heterogeneous robots, achieving superior exploration efficiency and communication savings compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [366] [Real-Time Non-Contact Force Compensation for Wrist-Mounted Force/Torque Sensors in Haptic-Enabled Robotic Surgery Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23696)
*Walid Shaker,Mustafa Suphi Erden*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于递归最小二乘（RLS）的实时补偿方法，用于低成本的机器人手术训练系统RoboScope中的腕部力/力矩传感器，有效消除非接触力干扰，实现95%以上的力补偿误差降低和91%以上的力矩补偿误差降低，为触觉反馈训练提供了可靠的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉反馈在机器人辅助手术中长期缺失，而现有商用机器人系统的触觉技术成本高昂，限制了其在训练和研究中的可及性。因此，需要一种低成本方案来实现可靠的触觉反馈训练。

Method: 在低成本手术训练系统RoboScope上集成腕部力/力矩传感器，并提出基于递归最小二乘（RLS）的实时补偿方法，以消除重力、传感器偏置、安装偏移及相关扭矩等非接触力干扰，无需数据集采集和频繁重新校准，且能自适应变化的操作条件。

Result: 实验验证表明，该方法在非接触力补偿上实现了超过95%的误差降低，非接触扭矩补偿上实现超过91%的误差降低，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为机器人手术训练和研究中的可靠触觉反馈提供了有效且低成本的解决方案，具有重要的应用潜力。

Abstract: Haptic feedback has been a long-missed feature in robotic-assisted surgery, one that would allow surgeons to perceive tissue properties and apply controlled forces during delicate procedures. Although commercial robotic systems have begun to integrate haptic technologies, their high costs limit accessibility for training and research purposes. To address this gap, we extend our previously developed low-cost robotic surgery training setup, RoboScope, by incorporating a wrist-mounted force/torque (F/T) sensor for haptic feedback training. Wrist-mounted sensing avoids many challenges associated with tip-mounted sensors but introduces additional non-contact forces, such as gravity, sensor bias, installation offsets, and associated torques, which compromise measurement accuracy. In this paper, we propose a robust real-time compensation method based on recursive least squares (RLS). This method eliminates the need for dataset collection and frequent recalibration while adapting to changing operating conditions. Experimental validation demonstrates that the proposed approach achieves over 95% error reduction in non-contact force compensation and more than 91% in non-contact torque compensation, significantly outperforming existing methods. These results highlight the potential of our approach for providing reliable haptic feedback in robotic surgery training and research.

</details>


### [367] [Unleashing the Agility of Wheeled-Legged Robots for High-Dynamic Reflexive Obstacle Evasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23761)
*Yongen Zhao,Zihao Xu,Wenzhi Lu,Zhen Chu,Ce Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AWARE（自适应轮腿式避障与反射性规避）框架，一种分层强化学习方法，使轮腿机器人在高速动态障碍物面前实现敏捷规避，包含前冲和侧闪等步态，经仿真和实物实验验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮腿机器人虽兼具轮式高效与腿式适应能力，但面对快速移动障碍物时，由于混合形态、模态耦合和非完整约束的限制，难以实现高动态的反射性规避。

Method: 提出AWARE分层强化学习框架，利用层次化策略架构使机器人自主涌现前冲、侧闪等多种规避步态，充分发挥混合形态的敏捷性潜力。

Result: 在Isaac Lab仿真及M20平台实物实验中，AWARE在多种动态场景下实现了鲁棒且敏捷的避障，并展现出行为各异的规避策略。

Conclusion: AWARE框架有效提升了轮腿机器人的反射性敏捷避障能力，验证了轮腿机器人固有的反射性敏捷潜力。

Abstract: Wheeled-legged robots combine the energy efficiency of wheeled locomotion with the terrain adaptability of legged systems, making them promising platforms for agile mobility in complex and dynamic environments. However, enabling high-dynamic reflexive evasion against fast-moving obstacles remains challenging due to the hybrid morphology, mode coupling, and non-holonomic constraints of such platforms. In this work, we propose AWARE, Adaptive Wheeled-Legged Avoidance and Reflexive Evasion, a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework for high-dynamic obstacle avoidance in wheeled-legged robots. The proposed system naturally exhibits diverse emergent gaits and evasive behaviors, including forward lunge and lateral dodge, thereby leveraging the robot's hybrid morphology to enhance agility under highly dynamic threats. Extensive experiments in Isaac Lab simulation and real-world deployment on the M20 platform across diverse dynamic scenarios demonstrate that AWARE achieves robust and agile obstacle avoidance while revealing behaviorally distinct evasive strategies. These results highlight both the practical effectiveness of AWARE and the intrinsic reflexive agility of wheeled-legged robots.

</details>


### [368] [Vision-Language-Action Safety: Threats, Challenges, Evaluations, and Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23775)
*Qi Li,Bo Yin,Weiqi Huang,Ruhao Liu,Bojun Zou,Runpeng Yu,Jingwen Ye,Weihao Yu,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统性地综述了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在具身智能中的安全挑战，沿攻击时机（训练时/推理时）与防御时机（训练时/推理时）两条平行时间轴组织文献，涵盖攻击、防御、评估和部署四个维度，并指出了认证鲁棒性、物理可实现防御、安全感知训练等关键开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型作为具身智能的统一范式正快速兴起，但其具身特性带来了不可逆物理后果、多模态攻击面、实时延迟约束、长程轨迹误差传播及数据供应链漏洞等新型安全挑战，而现有文献分散在机器人学习、对抗机器学习、AI对齐和自主系统安全等多个独立领域，缺乏统一视角。

Method: 采用综述方法，沿攻击时机（训练时 vs. 推理时）和防御时机（训练时 vs. 推理时）两条平行时间轴组织文献，从攻击（数据投毒、后门、对抗补丁、跨模态扰动、语义越狱、冻结攻击）、防御（训练时和运行时防御）、评估（基准和指标）和部署（六个部署领域）四个视角系统梳理现有研究。

Result: 明确了VLA安全区别于纯文本LLM安全和经典机器人安全的独特范畴；系统分类了训练时和推理时的各类威胁及对应防御；分析了现有基准和评估指标；讨论了六类部署领域的安全挑战；识别出认证鲁棒性、物理可实现防御、安全感知训练、统一运行时安全架构和标准化评估等关键开放问题。

Conclusion: VLA安全是一个亟待系统研究的交叉领域，需要在具身智能的独特约束下设计专门的安全方法，未来工作应重点关注认证鲁棒性形式化方法、物理世界可部署的防御机制、安全与任务性能的联合训练范式、实时多模态安全监控架构以及标准化的评估体系。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are emerging as a unified substrate for embodied intelligence. This shift raises a new class of safety challenges, stemming from the embodied nature of VLA systems, including irreversible physical consequences, a multimodal attack surface across vision, language, and state, real-time latency constraints on defense, error propagation over long-horizon trajectories, and vulnerabilities in the data supply chain. Yet the literature remains fragmented across robotic learning, adversarial machine learning, AI alignment, and autonomous systems safety. This survey provides a unified and up-to-date overview of safety in Vision-Language-Action models. We organize the field along two parallel timing axes, attack timing (training-time vs. inference-time and defense timing (training-time vs. inference-time, linking each class of threat to the stage at which it can be mitigated. We first define the scope of VLA safety, distinguishing it from text-only LLM safety and classical robotic safety, and review the foundations of VLA models, including architectures, training paradigms, and inference mechanisms. We then examine the literature through four lenses: Attacks, Defenses, Evaluation, and Deployment. We survey training-time threats such as data poisoning and backdoors, as well as inference-time attacks including adversarial patches, cross-modal perturbations, semantic jailbreaks, and freezing attacks. We review training-time and runtime defenses, analyze existing benchmarks and metrics, and discuss safety challenges across six deployment domains. Finally, we highlight key open problems, including certified robustness for embodied trajectories, physically realizable defenses, safety-aware training, unified runtime safety architectures, and standardized evaluation.

</details>


### [369] [Cooptimizing Safety and Performance Using Safety Value-Constrained Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23863)
*Hao Wang,Nam Nguyen,Armand Jordana,Ludovic Righetti,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合安全价值函数的模型预测控制（MPC）方法，通过在终端约束中引入控制不变安全集，实现了高性能与可证明的安全性保证，并在Flexiv Rizon 10s机械臂上通过仿真和硬件实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MPC的有限规划窗口无法保证超出规划范围的安全性，而传统的终端集构造依赖局部线性化或保守近似，导致安全性保证过于保守或表达能力不足。

Method: 在MPC的终端约束中引入基于安全价值函数的控制不变安全集，确保每个规划周期结束时系统状态属于该安全集；利用可达性分析构建安全价值函数，避免了传统方法的局部线性化或保守近似。

Result: 在Flexiv Rizon 10s机械臂上的仿真和硬件实验表明，该方法相比标准状态约束MPC和反应式安全滤波，在约束满足度和鲁棒性上均有提升，同时保持竞争力的任务性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法在精确安全价值函数和可行初始条件下具有递归可行性，从而确保持续的安全性；相比传统终端集方法，提供了更少保守性、更具表达力的安全性保证。

Abstract: Autonomous systems are increasingly deployed in real-world environments, where they must achieve high performance while maintaining safety under state and input constraints. Although Model Predictive Control (MPC) provides a principled framework for constrained optimal control, guaranteeing safety beyond its finite planning horizon remains a fundamental challenge. In this work, we augment MPC with a safety value function-based terminal constraint that enforces membership in a control-invariant safe set at the end of each planning horizon. This formulation enables real-time synthesis of trajectories that are both high-performing and provably safe. We show that, under an exact safety value function and a feasible initialization, the proposed MPC scheme is recursively feasible, thereby ensuring persistent safety. In contrast to traditional terminal set constructions that rely on local linearizations or conservative approximations, our approach incorporates a reachability-based safety value function for terminal constraints, yielding less conservative and more expressive safety guarantees. We validate the proposed framework through simulation and hardware experiments on a Flexiv Rizon 10s manipulator. Results demonstrate improved constraint satisfaction and robustness compared to standard state-constrained MPC and reactive safety filtering, while maintaining competitive task performance. The full implementation and experiments are available on the project website.

</details>


### [370] [Multi-Robot Motions in Milliseconds: Vector-Accelerated Primitives for Sampling-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23960)
*James D. Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将向量加速运动规划(VAMP)框架扩展至多机器人运动规划(MRMP)，开发了两个向量加速原语(MotVal和FFC)，在运动验证中实现超1100倍加速，在多种MRMP算法中实现超850倍规划时间加速，部分场景可在毫秒级求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将VAMP框架从单机器人扩展到多机器人领域，利用SIMD并行加速多机器人运动规划，解决MRMP计算效率低下的问题。

Method: 开发了两个向量加速原语：多机器人运动验证(MotVal)和首次冲突查找(FFC)，利用SIMD并行加速多机器人域的计算；并修改了多个代表性MRMP算法以使用这些原语。

Result: 纯多机器人运动验证测试中实现了超1100倍加速；在多种MRMP算法（操作臂、刚体、异构团队）中，规划时间加速超850倍，部分场景毫秒级求解。

Conclusion: 向量加速方法可以有效扩展到多机器人运动规划领域，大幅提升计算效率，有望实现实时多机器人规划。

Abstract: In this paper, we extend the recent Vector-Accelerated Motion Planning (VAMP) framework to multi-robot motion planning (MRMP). We develop two vector-accelerated primitives, multi-robot MotionValidation (MotVal) and FindFirstConflict (FFC), which exploit SIMD parallelism within the multi-robot domain. On pure multi-robot motion validation tests, this achieves over 1100X speedup in validation time. Additionally, we modify a representative set of MRMP algorithms to use these new primitives. The relative speedup for each algorithm is studied on scenarios with manipulator, rigid body, and heterogeneous teams with some instances producing multi-robot solutions in the order of milliseconds and, in many cases, shows planning time speedups of over 850X.

</details>


### [371] [Designing Robots to Support Parent-Child Connections: Opportunities Through Robot-Mediated Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23976)
*Michael F Xu,Bengisu Cagiltay,Yaxin Hu,Anjun Zhu,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探索了机器人作为家庭沟通中介的可能性，通过两项研究（6个家庭的居家技术探测和20个家庭的实验室研究）分析了机器人的行为策略（被动、反应、主动）和沟通模式（同步、异步）如何影响亲子互动与连接感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术进步常取代人际互动，而家庭连接感对成员福祉和家庭功能有积极作用。作者希望探索机器人促进的沟通工具能否创造新的家庭连接机会，而非替代人与人之间的交流。

Method: 两项研究：1) 与6个有5-12岁孩子的家庭进行居家技术探测，探索设计空间；2) 与20个家庭进行实验室研究，系统考察机器人的行为策略（被动、反应、主动）和沟通模式（同步、异步）两个交互设计维度对亲子互动的影响。

Result: 揭示了父母和儿童如何挪用机器人中介的交流方式，他们在主动性、时机和隐私方面体验到的张力，以及他们对支持日常连接所设想的机遇。

Conclusion: 机器人可以通过不同的行为策略和沟通模式设计，为家庭连接创造新的可能性，但需注意解决用户在主动性、时机和隐私方面的顾虑。

Abstract: The sense of family connectedness may support positive outcomes including individual well-being, resilience, and healthy family functioning. However, as technologies advance, they often replace human-human interactions instead of nurturing them. In this work, we investigate how robot-facilitated communication tools might instead create new opportunities for family connection. We conducted two studies with families with children aged 5-12. We first explored the design space through in-home technology probe sessions with six families. These probes inspired us to explore two key interaction design dimensions: the robot's behavior strategy (passive, reactive, proactive) and the mode of communication (synchronous, asynchronous). We then conducted a laboratory study with 20 families to examine how the two dimensions shaped parent-child interaction and connection. Our findings characterize how parents and children appropriated robot-mediated exchanges, the tensions they experienced around initiative, timing, and privacy, and the opportunities they envisioned for supporting everyday connectedness.

</details>


### [372] [Supporting Family-School Partnerships with Robot-Facilitated Home-Based Activities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23978)
*Michael F Xu,Qiyao Yang,Heather Kirkorian,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探索了将社交机器人引入家庭环境以支持家校合作的新方法，通过访谈和共同设计开发了机器人系统，并在10个家庭中进行为期一周的实地研究，分析了家庭如何将机器人融入日常生活、家长引导风格的影响以及机器人的实用性与挑战，提供了实证见解、模块化系统及设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家校合作对儿童发展至关重要，但家庭常面临时间限制、沟通碎片化及缺乏有意义的参与机会等障碍，因此需要探索新技术手段来促进更广泛的家校合作。

Method: 通过访谈和共同设计会话，与家长和儿童共同设计并开发了支持家庭中关于学校话题沟通的机器人系统；随后在10个家庭中进行了为期一周的实地研究评估。

Result: 家庭将机器人融入日常生活；家长引导风格影响了机器人的使用方式；用户对机器人的实用性和挑战有不同感知。

Conclusion: 研究提供了实证见解、模块化系统以及家庭-儿童-机器人交互的设计启示，讨论了伦理和隐私考量，拓展了支持家校合作的技术设计空间。

Abstract: Family-school partnerships (FSP) are critical to children's development, yet families often face barriers such as time constraints, fragmented communication, and limited opportunities for meaningful engagement. As a step toward facilitating broader family-school partnerships, we explore a novel approach that integrates a social robot into family settings, specifically supporting home-based activities. Through interviews and co-design sessions, we designed and developed a robotic system informed by both parents and children, that supported, among other interactions, family communication about school topics. We evaluated the robot in a week-long, in-home study with 10 families. Our findings show how families integrated the robot into daily life, how parental facilitation styles shaped use, and how families perceived both the helpfulness and challenges of the robot. We contribute empirical insights, a modular system, and design implications for family- and child-robot interactions. We discuss ethical and privacy considerations, and broaden the design space for technologies supporting family-school partnerships.

</details>


### [373] [Betting for Sim-to-Real Performance Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24018)
*Zaid Mahboob,Yujia Chen,Bowen Weng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于赌博机制（betting）的机器人性能评估方法，用于在物理实验受限条件下，通过模拟器数据高效、准确地估计真实世界行为，并从理论上证明了该方法优于传统蒙特卡洛估计，提供了实用近似方案与决策规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人真实世界测试昂贵、耗时且受安全限制，导致物理实验数据稀缺；现有方差缩减（如重要性采样）或偏差校正（如预测驱动推断）方法虽有效但仍有局限，亟需一种互补且理论严谨的新评估框架。

Method: 从赌博视角审视性能评估问题，建立赌博机制的理论条件以证明其能产生比蒙特卡洛估计更精确高效的估计；设计了理论上有保障且可实际实现的近似最优赌注，并给出诊断近似策略是否有效的具体决策规则。

Result: 通过合成示例和交叉保真度计算模拟器验证了方法的有效性；特别展示了利用合成分布推断真实机器人抓取放置精度的案例，表明该赌博视角下的模拟到现实迁移方法具有自然性和可行性。

Conclusion: 提出的赌博框架为模拟到现实迁移中的性能评估提供了一种新颖且理论坚实的工具，能够更高效地利用有限真实数据，在算法基准测试、控制器验证及认证决策中具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of robot performance evaluation, focusing on how to obtain accurate and efficient estimates of real-world behavior under severe constraints on physical experimentation. Such estimates are essential for benchmarking algorithms, comparing design alternatives, validating controllers, and supporting certification or regulatory decision-making, yet real-world testing with physical robots is often expensive, time-consuming, and safety-limited. To mitigate the scarcity of real-world trials, sim-to-real methodologies are commonly employed, using low-cost simulators to inform, supplement, or prioritize physical experiments. Departing from (and complementary to) existing approaches in variance reduction (e.g., importance-sampling variants) or bias-correction (e.g., through prediction-powered inference or learned control variates), we examine this performance-evaluation problem through the lens of betting. We establish theoretical conditions under which a betting mechanism can yield accurate and efficient estimates (provably outperforming the Monte Carlo estimator) and we characterize how such bets should be constructed. We further develop theoretically grounded yet practically implementable approximations of the ideal bet, and we provide concrete decision rules that diagnose when these approximate betting strategies are working as intended. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods using both synthetic examples and cross-fidelity computational simulators. Notably, we also showcase an illustrative case in which a group of synthetic distributions are used to infer the real-world pick-and-place accuracy of a robotic manipulator, a seemingly unconventional sim-to-real transfer that becomes natural and feasible under the proposed betting perspective. Programs for reproducing empirical results are available at https://github.com/ISUSAIL/Bet4Sim2Real.

</details>


### [374] [Event-based SLAM Benchmark for High-Speed Maneuvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24033)
*Sheng Zhong,Junkai Niu,Guillermo Gallego,Kaizhen Sun,Yang Yi,Zhiqiang Miao,Dewen Hu,Yaonan Wang,Davide Scaramuzza,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分析了现有事件相机状态估计方法在高速机动场景下的局限性，指出现有数据集和评估方法的不足，并提出了一个名为EvSLAM的基准框架，包含多样化的数据采集平台、极端光照场景、广泛的高速运动模式以及新的评估指标，对SOTA方法进行了全面评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有微秒级时间分辨率和低延迟优势，理论上适合高速机动场景下的视觉状态估计。然而现有方法在任意剧烈机动下的六自由度状态估计问题尚未被充分解决，且缺乏统一的基准测试框架来公平评估各方法的实际性能极限。

Method: 1) 对现有SOTA事件相机VO/VIO方法进行了深入分析；2) 指出现有公开数据集的不足；3) 构建了EvSLAM基准框架，包含多样化的数据采集平台、极端光照场景、广泛的挑战性运动模式；4) 提出了针对移动机器人高速机动的明确定义和新的评估指标。

Result: 通过对SOTA事件相机状态估计方法的基准测试，揭示出最优架构以及当前领域面临的主要挑战。

Conclusion: 当前事件相机在任意剧烈机动下的状态估计问题尚未完全解决，EvSLAM框架为公平评估和推动该领域发展提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Event-based cameras are bio-inspired sensors with pixels that independently and asynchronously respond to brightness changes at microsecond resolution, offering the potential to handle visual tasks in high-speed maneuvering scenarios. Existing event-based approaches, although successful in mitigating motion blur caused by high-speed maneuvers, suffer from many limitations. Some of them highlight a success of pose tracking for a fronto-parallel fast shaking camera closed to the structure, while others assume pure (optionally aggressive) three-degree-of-freedom rotations. The former requires persistent local map visibility within the field of view (FOV), whereas the latter fails to generalize to six-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) motions where both linear and angular velocities may be large. Consequently, current successes do not fully demonstrate that event-based state estimation under arbitrary aggressive maneuvers is a fully solved problem. To quantitatively assess the extent to which the potential of event cameras has been unlocked, we conduct a thorough analysis of state-of-the-art (SOTA) event-based visual odometry (VO)/visual-inertial odometry (VIO) methods and report shortcomings in current public datasets. Furthermore, we introduce a benchmarking framework for event-based state estimation, called EvSLAM, characterized by sufficient variation in data collection platforms, diverse extreme lighting scenarios, and a wide scope of challenging motion patterns under a clear and rigorous definition of high-speed maneuvers for mobile robots, along with a novel evaluation metric designed to fairly assess the operational limits of event-based solutions. This framework benchmarks state-of-the-art methods, yielding insights into optimal architectures and persistent challenges.

</details>


### [375] [Trajectory Planning for an Articulated Commercial Vehicle using Model Predictive Contouring Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24064)
*A. J. Aertssen,R. G. M. Huisman,I. J. M. Besselink,J. Elfring,M. J. G. van de Molengraft*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对铰接式商用车辆（牵引车-半挂车），提出了一种基于模型预测等约束控制（MPCC）的轨迹规划方法，通过引入场景依赖的锚点权重和明确的路边界约束，成功实现了前向和倒车场景下的轨迹规划，并在全尺寸原型车上验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MPCC方法主要适用于乘用车，但牵引车-半挂车体积更大、半挂车路径与牵引车不一致、倒车时容易发生折叠失稳，且实际驾驶场景中需要根据场景对不同车辆锚点（如半挂车在泊车时、牵引车在充电时）赋予不同优先级，因此需要扩展MPCC方法以满足这些特殊需求。

Method: 扩展MPCC框架，引入场景依赖的锚点权重机制，允许根据不同驾驶场景动态调整牵引车和半挂车位置的优先级；同时加入显式的道路边界约束，覆盖前、后牵引车轴和半挂车轴，确保所有车轮保持在可行驶区域内。

Result: 仿真结果表明该方法能够在代表性物流场景中成功实现前向和倒车导航；分析了优化参数对轨迹的影响，揭示了控制车辆行为的关键因素；全尺寸原型车的初步测试结果验证了方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于MPCC的轨迹规划方法有效解决了铰接式商用车辆在复杂场景下的轨迹规划问题，特别是倒车时的稳定性控制和场景依赖的锚点优先级调节，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a trajectory planning method for articulated commercial vehicles, specifically tractor-semitrailers, based on Model Predictive Contouring Control (MPCC). Although MPCC has proven effective for passenger cars, it is generally ill-suited for tractor-semitrailers. These vehicles are significantly larger, the semitrailer follows a different path than the tractor, and reversing maneuvers are unstable and prone to jackknifing. Furthermore, practical driving scenarios often require scenario-dependent prioritization of different vehicle `anchor points', e.g., prioritizing the semitrailer position during docking or the tractor position when parking to charge. Therefore, we extend MPCC to enable scenario-dependent weighting of these anchor points and incorporate explicit road-boundary constraints for the front and rear tractor axles and the semitrailer axle, thereby ensuring that all considered wheels remain within the drivable area. The simulation results demonstrate the successful navigation of a representative logistic scenario in both forward and reverse direction. Furthermore, the influence of the optimization parameters on the trajectories is analyzed, providing insights into controlling the vehicle behavior. Finally, first tests using a full-scale prototype vehicle show the practical applicability of the approach.

</details>


### [376] [OpenPodcar2: a robust, ROS2 vehicle for self-driving research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24242)
*Rakshit Soni,Chris Waltham,Md Umar Ibrahim,Mark Crampton,Charles Fox*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenPodcar2是一个基于商用硬顶代步车改造的ROS2接口开源自主车辆平台，成本约2,000-7,000美元，速度可达15km/h，集成了ROS2接口、Gazebo仿真、nav2导航堆栈（含SLAM和路径规划），可用于最后一英里自动驾驶出租车或货物运输等研究与应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自主车辆研究中，现有平台往往在成本、安全性、鲁棒性和实际部署可行性之间难以取得平衡。需要一个低成本的、开源的、可复现的自主车辆平台，既能满足实验室研究需求，又具备一定的实际部署能力。

Method: 基于商用硬顶代步车进行改造，采用开源硬件和软件设计：(a) 提供详细的物料清单和组装说明；(b) 集成通用OSH R4机电控制板，并构建Gazebo仿真模型，两者共用ROS2接口；(c) 实现基于nav2堆栈的自主规划与控制，包括SLAM和避障导航。

Result: 成功搭建了OpenPodcar2平台，可载人或等效负载以15km/h速度行驶；总构建成本约7,000美元（全新部件）或2,000美元（二手代步车）；尺寸足够小且安全，可停放于标准研究实验室，同时具备一定部署场景的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: OpenPodcar2在实际效用、安全性、成本和鲁棒性之间取得了良好的研究平衡，为自主车辆研究社区提供了一个低门槛、可复现的开源平台。

Abstract: OpenPodcar2 is a robust, ROS2-interfaced, low-cost, open source hardware and software, autonomous vehicle platform based on an off-the-shelf, hard-canopy, mobility scooter donor vehicle. It is a modification of the previous OpenPodcar design, which extends it with robust electronics and ROS2 interfacing, to enable both research and also potential deployment use cases. The platform consists of (a) hardware components: documented as a bill of materials and build instructions; (b) integration to the general purpose OSH R4 mechatronics board and a Gazebo simulation of the vehicle, both presenting a common ROS2 interface (c) higher-level ROS2 software implementations and configurations of standard robot autonomous planning and control, including the nav2 stack which performs SLAM and enacts commands to drive the vehicle from a current to a desired pose around obstacles. OpenPodcar2 can transport a human passenger or similar load at speeds up to 15km/h, for example for use as a last-mile autonomous taxi service or to transport delivery containers similarly around a city center. It is small and safe enough to be parked in a standard research lab robust enough for some deployment cases. Total build cost was around 7,000USD from new components, or 2,000USD with a used Donor Vehicle. OpenPodcar2 thus provides a research balance between real world utility, safety, cost and robustness.

</details>


### [377] [Projected Attainable Speed Space: A Driving Efficiency Metric Connecting Instantaneous Evaluation to Travel Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24295)
*Xiaohua Zhao,Zhaowei Huang,Chen Chen,Haiyi Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出投影可达速度空间（PASS）模型，通过融合运动学和空间交通信息，统一了自动驾驶中瞬时与行程层面的驾驶效率评估。该模型由可用加速空间及其利用率两个耦合要素构成，经仿真实验数据标定，在10次换道事件中平均决定系数达0.913，验证了跨尺度一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有瞬时效率指标（如速度、相对速度、车间距离）难以捕捉交通上下文，且无法保证瞬时输出与行程级结果之间的一致性，亟需一个统一框架来实现跨时间尺度的驾驶效率评估。

Method: PASS模型通过理想化追赶机动推导投影可达速度，定义可用加速空间；以可用加速空间的时序变化表征利用率；通过时间聚合PASS定义行程级效率指标，并利用驾驶仿真实验轨迹数据进行参数标定以最大化与行程时间的一致性。

Result: 在10次换道事件中，时间聚合PASS与观测行程时间之间的平均决定系数为0.913，验证了该模型在瞬时与行程层面之间的一致性评估能力。

Conclusion: PASS模型提供了一个统一的、基于物理的框架，能够同时支持自动驾驶中的实时决策与长期性能分析。

Abstract: Inefficient driving behaviors, such as overly conservative yielding, remain a key obstacle to deployment of autonomous vehicles (AVs). Instantaneous driving efficiency metrics are crucial for self-driving decision-making because they affect real-time performance evaluation and control optimization. However, commonly used indicators, including speed, relative speed, and inter-vehicle distance, are limited in capturing traffic context and in ensuring consistency between instantaneous outputs and travel-level outcomes. This study proposes the Projected Attainable Speed Space (PASS) model, a unified framework for driving efficiency assessment across instantaneous and travel-level analyses by integrating kinematic and spatial traffic information. PASS characterizes instantaneous driving efficiency with two coupled elements: potential for speed improvement (available acceleration space) and response to that potential (utilization of available acceleration space). Available acceleration space is referenced to projected attainable speed, derived from an idealized catch-up maneuver using relative speed and spacing to the leading vehicle; utilization is represented by the temporal change in available acceleration space. To ensure cross-scale consistency, time-aggregated PASS is defined as a travel-level efficiency metric. Trajectory data from a driving simulation experiment are used for parameter calibration to maximize agreement between time-aggregated PASS and observed travel times. Across 10 lane-change events, results show strong consistency, with an average coefficient of determination of 0.913, validating PASS for consistent efficiency evaluation across instantaneous and travel-level temporal scales. This study provides a unified, physically grounded framework that supports real-time decision-making and long-term performance analysis in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [378] [Pedestrians play chicken with an autonomous vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24384)
*Rakshit Soni,Charles Fox*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次在真实自动驾驶车辆上通过人类受试者实验验证了博弈论中的序贯鸡模型，证明自动驾驶车辆可通过可信的微小碰撞威胁或侵入个人空间的风险来缓解"冻结机器人问题"，行人行为与模型拟合良好，且其时间价值较低，表明行人会规划避免接近个人空间惩罚和实际碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆通常被编程为无条件让行行人以确保安全，但这会导致"冻结机器人问题"——行人学会在每次交互中主张优先权，使车辆停滞不前。需要找到一种方法让AV在保证安全的同时也能有效通行。

Method: 采用博弈论中的序贯鸡模型，在真实自动驾驶车辆上进行人类受试者实验，在实验约束的安全条件下观察行人行为，并用模型拟合数据。

Result: 行人行为可以被序贯鸡模型很好地拟合，且碰撞的时间价值较低，表明行人不仅规划避免实际碰撞，也会避免接近个人空间（proxemic personal space）的惩罚。

Conclusion: 自动驾驶车辆可以通过发出可信的微小碰撞威胁或侵入个人空间的风险来解决冻结机器人问题，这一博弈论方法在真实场景中有效。

Abstract: Automated vehicles (AVs) are commonly programmed to yield unconditionally to pedestrians in the interest of safety. However, this design choice can give rise to the Freezing Robot Problem in which pedestrians learn to assert priority at every interaction, causing vehicles to stall and make no progress. The game theoretic Sequential Chicken model has shown that, like human drivers, AVs can resolve this problem by trading credible threats of very small risks of collision or larger risks of less severe invasion of personal space against the value of time due to yielding delays. This paper presents the first demonstration and evaluation of this approach using a real AV with human subjects and shows that pedestrian behavior under experimentally constrained safety conditions can be well fitted by Sequential Chicken, with a low time value of collision, suggestive of their planning to avoid proxemic personal space penalties as well as actual collisions.

</details>


### [379] [FreqCache: Accelerating Embodied VLN Models with Adaptive Frequency-Guided Token Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24391)
*Zihao Zheng,Xingyue Zhou,Zhihao Mao,Songyu Sun,Lingyue Zhang,Yulong Ao,Yupu Feng,Qiongqiong Zhang,Yonghua Lin,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLN模型计算开销大及现有token缓存方法在视觉域中的三大挑战（视角迁移失效、忽略边缘信息、缺乏时序自适应调整），提出频域引导的token缓存框架FreqCache，利用频域特性实现1.59倍加速且开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN模型导航精度高但计算开销大；现有视觉域的token缓存方法在VLN模型中面临三大挑战：视角迁移时失效、缺少边缘信息、缺乏时序自适应调整能力。

Method: 提出频域引导的token缓存框架FreqCache，利用频域的不变性和可分析性，实现最优token缓存的建立、刷新和自适应调整。

Result: FreqCache实现1.59倍加速，且额外开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 频域方法可有效解决视觉域token缓存在VLN中的困境，达到高效加速且低开销的效果。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Navigation (VLN) models exhibit excellent navigation accuracy but incur high computational overhead. Token caching has emerged as a promising training-free strategy to reduce this cost by reusing token computation results; however, existing token caching approaches rely on visual domain methods for cacheable token selection, leading to challenges when adapted to VLN models. 1) Visual domain methods become invalid when there is viewpoint migration. 2) Visual domain methods neglect critical edge information without the aid of additional algorithms. 3) Visual domain methods overlook the temporal variation of scenarios and lack adjustability in cache budgets. In this paper, we develop detailed analyses and find that the impacts of these challenges exhibit invariance and analyzability in the frequency domain. Based on these, we propose a frequency-guided token caching framework, called FreqCache. Utilizing the inherent properties of the frequency domain, FreqCache achieves optimal token cache establishment, refreshment, and adaptive adjustment. Experiments show that FreqCache achieves 1.59x speedup with ignorable overhead, showing the effect of integrating frequency domain methods in VLN token caching.

</details>


### [380] [Characterizing Vision-Language-Action Models across XPUs: Constraints and Acceleration for On-Robot Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24447)
*Kaijun Zhou,Qiwei Chen,Da Peng,Zhiyang Li,Xijun Li,Jinyu Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在异构边缘加速器（GPU/XPU/NPU）上的部署，通过模型-硬件协同特征化，揭示了VLA推理的两阶段模式（计算密集的VLM骨干+内存密集的动作专家），并提出DP-Cache和V-AEFusion两种优化方法，分别在GPU和边缘NPU上实现最高2.9倍和6倍的速度提升，且成功率仅轻微下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型评估多基于桌面级GPU，忽略了异构边缘加速器带来的权衡与机会，而真实机器人部署受限于实时性、成本和能耗预算，亟需对低成本VLA部署进行系统性分析。

Method: 1) 构建跨加速器排行榜，在CET（成本、能耗、时间）指标下评估模型-硬件组合；2) 通过深度性能剖析，发现VLA推理的两阶段模式（计算密集的VLM骨干+内存密集的动作专家）及其导致的阶段相关硬件利用不足；3) 提出DP-Cache（减少扩散冗余）和V-AEFusion（异步流水线并行）两种优化方法。

Result: 右尺寸的边缘设备在成本/能耗效率上可超越旗舰级GPU，同时满足控制速率约束；DP-Cache和V-AEFusion在GPU上实现最高2.9倍加速，在边缘NPU上实现最高6倍加速，且成功率仅轻微下降。

Conclusion: 通过模型-硬件协同特征化，可以识别VLA部署的关键瓶颈，并针对性地设计优化策略，使边缘设备在成本、能耗和实时性方面成为可行甚至更优的选择。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are promising for generalist robot control, but on-robot deployment is bottlenecked by real-time inference under tight cost and energy budgets. Most prior evaluations rely on desktop-grade GPUs, obscuring the trade-offs and opportunities offered by heterogeneous edge accelerators (GPUs/XPUs/NPUs). We present a systematic analysis for low-cost VLA deployment via model-hardware co-characterization. First, we build a cross-accelerator leaderboard and evaluate model-hardware pairs under CET (Cost, Energy, Time), showing that right-sized edge devices can be more cost-/energy-efficient than flagship GPUs while meeting control-rate constraints. Second, using in-depth profiling, we uncover a consistent two-phase inference pattern: a compute-bound VLM backbone followed by a memory-bound Action Expert, which induces phase-dependent underutilization and hardware inefficiency. Finally, guided by these insights, we propose DP-Cache and V-AEFusion to reduce diffusion redundancy and enable asynchronous pipeline parallelism, achieving up to 2.9x speedup on GPUs and 6x on edge NPUs with only marginal success degradation. The example leaderboard website is available at: https://vla-leaderboard-01.vercel.app/.

</details>


### [381] [SPLIT: Separating Physical-Contact via Latent Arithmetic in Image-Based Tactile Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24449)
*Wadhah Zai El Amri,Nicolás Navarro-Guerrero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPLIT提出了一种基于隐空间算术策略的触觉传感器仿真方法，通过解耦接触几何与光学属性，实现了跨传感器适配、快速推理、双向仿真等功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获取真实触觉交互数据因物理复杂性和多样性而困难，需要有效的触觉传感器仿真方法来加速机器人触觉感知研究。

Method: 采用隐空间算术策略显式解耦接触几何与传感器光学属性；结合校准的有限元法软体网格仿真，支持可变分辨率；实现双向仿真（变形网格→触觉图像 和 触觉图像→网格重建）。

Result: 无需为每个新传感器单元重新校准即可适应不同DIGIT背景，可迁移数据到GelSight R1.5等不同传感器；推理速度优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SPLIT通过解耦和双向仿真能力，为机器人触觉感知研究提供了高效、灵活且可迁移的仿真工具。

Abstract: Training machine learning models for robotic tactile sensing requires vast amounts of data, yet obtaining realistic interaction data remains a challenge due to physical complexity and variability. Simulating tactile sensors is thus a crucial step in accelerating progress. This paper presents SPLIT, a novel method for simulating image-based tactile sensors, with a primary focus on the DIGIT sensor. Central to our approach is a latent space arithmetic strategy that explicitly disentangles contact geometry from sensor-specific optical properties. Unlike methods that require recalibration for every new unit, this disentanglement allows SPLIT to adapt to diverse DIGIT backgrounds and even transfer data to distinct sensors like the GelSight R1.5 without full model retraining. Beyond this adaptability, our approach achieves faster inference speeds than existing alternatives. Furthermore, we provide a calibrated finite element method (FEM) soft-body mesh simulation with variable resolution, offering a tunable trade-off between speed and fidelity. Additionally, our algorithm supports bidirectional simulation, allowing for both the generation of realistic images from deformation meshes and the reconstruction of meshes from tactile images. This versatility makes SPLIT a valuable tool for accelerating progress in robotic tactile sensing research.

</details>


### [382] [Guiding Vector Field Generation via Score-based Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24487)
*Zirui Chen,Shiliang Guo,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出分数引导向量场（SGVF），利用基于分数的生成模型从数据分布中构建引导向量场，解决了传统方法无法处理无序、多分支路径的问题，并在机器人导航实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统引导向量场假设路径是光滑有序曲线，无法处理无序、多分支或由概率模型生成的路径，需要一种统一框架来应对复杂拓扑结构。

Method: 利用基于分数的生成模型从点云数据中学习切向场，通过单位范数、正交性和方向一致性损失确保几何保真度和控制可行性，建立了扩散模型分数消失与GVF奇点之间的对应关系。

Result: 在平面环境机器人导航实验中，SGVF在经典GVF失效的场景下实现了可靠的路径跟随。

Conclusion: SGVF可作为生成建模与几何控制之间的桥梁，为复杂拓扑路径跟随提供统一解决方案。

Abstract: Guiding Vector Fields (GVFs) are a powerful tool for robotic path following. However, classical methods assume smooth, ordered curves and fail when paths are unordered, multi-branch, or generated by probabilistic models. We propose a unified framework, termed the Score-Induced Guiding Vector Field (SGVF), which leverages score-based generative modeling to construct vector fields directly from data distributions. SGVF learns tangent fields from point clouds with unit-norm, orthogonality, and directional-consistency losses, ensuring geometric fidelity and control feasibility. This approach removes the reliance on ad-hoc path segmentation and enables guidance along complex topologies such as branching and pseudo-manifolds. The study establishes a correspondence between score vanishing in diffusion models and GVF singularities and highlights representational capacity near sharp path curvatures. Experiments on robotic navigation in planar environments demonstrate that SGVF achieves reliable path following in scenarios where classical GVFs fail, underscoring its potential as a bridge between generative modeling and geometric control. Code and experiment video are available at https://github.com/czr-gif/Guiding-Vector-Field-Generation-via-Score-based-Diffusion-Model.

</details>


### [383] [Hybrid A*-Based Reverse Path-Planning of a Vehicle with Trailer System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24606)
*Xincheng Cao,Haochong Chen,Bilin Aksun-Guvenc,Levent Guvenc,Brian Link,Peter J Richmond,Dokyung Yim,Shihong Fan,John Harber*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的Hybrid A*算法，用于带拖车车辆的倒车泊车操作，该算法能够处理多体系统的运动学约束（包括防止折刀现象）并实现碰撞避免功能，通过仿真案例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类驾驶员操作带拖车车辆进行倒车泊车非常困难（多体系统、非直观控制），且现有路径规划方法缺乏碰撞避免功能，因此需要一种同时考虑路径规划和碰撞避免的算法。

Method: 基于改进的Hybrid A*算法，通过自适应调整可用转向输入限制（防止折刀现象）来适配车辆-拖车系统，并集成了碰撞避免功能以处理停车场中其他车辆和障碍物。

Result: 通过仿真案例研究证明了所提出方法的有效性，能够成功完成带拖车车辆的倒车泊车任务并避免碰撞。

Conclusion: 改进的Hybrid A*算法能够有效解决带拖车车辆倒车泊车中的路径规划与碰撞避免问题，具有适应性和实用性。

Abstract: Reverse parking maneuvering of a vehicle with trailer system is a difficult task to complete for human drivers due to the multi-body nature of the system and the unintuitive controls required to orientate the trailer properly. The problem is complicated with the presence of other vehicles that the trailer and its connected vehicle must avoid during the reverse parking maneuver. While path planning methods in reverse motion for vehicles with trailers exist, there is a lack of results that also offer collision avoidance as part of the algorithm. This paper hence proposes a modified Hybrid A*-based algorithm that can accommodate the vehicle-trailer system as well as collision avoidance considerations with the other vehicles and obstacles in the parking environment. One of the novelties of this proposed approach is its adaptability to the vehicle with trailer system, where limits of usable steering input that prevent the occurrence of jackknife incidents vary with respect to system configuration. The other contribution is the addition of the collision avoidance functionality which the standard Hybrid A* algorithm lacks. The method is developed and presented first, followed by simulation case studies to demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [384] [Real-time windrow detection from onboard tractor sensors for automated following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24628)
*Lorenz Gunreben,Nico Heider,Sebastian Zürner,Martin Schieck,Bogdan Franczyk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于自主饲草收割的多模态数据集（立体视觉+LiDAR），并实现了基于质心的实时草条跟随方法（>20 Hz）。实验表明，低成本立体传感器在4-10米关键范围内与LiDAR深度测量高度一致（0.965±0.021），为GPS-free草条检测提供了可复现的开源基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业草条检测系统的专有设计限制了透明度和开源自主饲草收割研究的进展。

Method: 1) 构建了包含立体视觉、LiDAR、GNSS轨迹的多模态数据集（ROS2 Humble格式）；2) 在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上实现了实时（>20 Hz）基于质心的草条跟随方法。

Result: 在4-10米关键引导范围内，立体视觉与LiDAR深度测量的一致性达到0.965±0.021，表明低成本立体传感器性能接近LiDAR。

Conclusion: 该开源ROS 2管道为无GPS草条检测提供了可复现的基准，支持实用化自主饲草收割系统的开发。

Abstract: Proprietary design in commercial windrow-detection systems restricts transparency and limits progress in open autonomous forage-harvesting research. We present a multi-modal dataset combining stereo vision and LiDAR from tractor-mounted sensors during real baling operations. The dataset includes synchronized sensor data with GNSS trajectories, partly released as ROS2 Humble bags on Zenodo, with additional data available on request. Using this dataset, we implement a real-time (>20 Hz) centroid-based windrow-following method on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin. Across the critical 4-10 m guidance range, stereo and LiDAR depth measurements show strong agreement (0.965 +/- 0.021), indicating that low-cost stereo sensors can approach LiDAR performance. Our open-source ROS 2 pipeline provides a reproducible benchmark for GPS-free windrow detection and supports development of practical autonomous forage-harvesting systems. Dataset: https://zenodo.org/records/17486318

</details>


### [385] [Computational Design and Co-Robotic Fabrication for Material Reuse in Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24648)
*Arash Adel,Daniel Ruan,Ruxin Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个集成框架，将数据驱动计算设计与自适应人机协作制造装配相结合，利用回收的异质木材（辅以新木材）实现非标准结构，并通过Timbrelyn案例验证了该方法在促进建筑循环利用和表达方面的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化和资源枯竭要求建筑行业从线性的"取-制-用-弃"模式转向循环低碳实践。材料再利用可减少原材料开采、减少废弃物并延长碳封存材料（如木材）的使用寿命，但实现这一潜力需要解决设计施工中处理异质、回收材料库存的技术和物流挑战。

Method: 提出一个集成框架，耦合数据驱动计算设计与反馈驱动的自适应人机协作制造装配系统，可处理不同长度和几何形状的回收木材，必要时辅以新木材，实现非标准结构的构建。

Result: 通过Timbrelyn案例研究安装项目验证了框架的有效性，证明了木材再利用可以启发并增强建筑表现力。

Conclusion: 这项工作推动了集成化设计到制造工作流程的发展，通过自适应反馈驱动方法应对库存约束和回收材料的不确定性，促进了材料再利用在新建筑设计与施工中的应用。

Abstract: Climate change and resource depletion demand a shift from the dominant linear "take-make-use-dispose" paradigm of construction toward circular, low-waste practices. Material reuse offers a promising pathway by reducing raw material extraction, mitigating waste, and extending the service lifespan of carbon-sequestering materials such as timber. Realizing this potential, however, requires addressing technical and logistical challenges across both design and construction for accommodating heterogeneous, reclaimed material inventories.
  This paper presents an integrated framework that couples data-driven computational design with feedback-driven adaptive human-robot collaborative (co-robotic) fabrication and assembly to enable the realization of nonstandard structures made from reclaimed timber of varying length and geometries, supplemented with new off-the-shelf timber when necessary. The framework is validated through Timbrelyn, a built case-study installation that demonstrates how timber reuse can inform and enhance architectural expression. This work contributes to the development of integrated design-to-fabrication workflows that advance adaptive, feedback-driven methods to handle inventory constraints and reclaimed material uncertainties, facilitating material reuse in the design and construction of new buildings and structures.

</details>


### [386] [Agent-Centric Visual Reinforcement Learning under Dynamic Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24661)
*Zhengru Fang,Yu Guo,Fei Liu,Yuang Zhang,Yihang Tao,Senkang Hu,Wenbo Ding,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VDCS基准（基于DeepMind Control Suite的马尔可夫切换视觉退化环境），证明现有视觉RL方法在动态扰动下性能严重下降，并从信息论角度揭示其根源在于重建目标将扰动伪影纠缠到潜在表征中。为此提出ACO-MoE框架，通过智能体中心观测与混合专家机制实现扰动解耦和任务相关前景提取，在VDCS上恢复95.3%的干净性能，并在DMControl泛化任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉强化学习面对动态视觉扰动（如不可预测的退化类型切换）时表现脆弱，现有方法缺乏针对此类非平稳扰动的系统性研究与有效鲁棒方案。

Method: 1) 构建VDCS基准，引入马尔可夫切换退化模拟真实世界非平稳扰动；2) 通过信息论分析证明重建目标导致扰动伪影纠缠；3) 提出ACO-MoE框架，利用智能体中心观测和混合专家机制，包含恢复专家（去除扰动）和前景提取专家（提取任务相关前景），在进入RL智能体前解耦感知与扰动。

Result: 在VDCS上恢复95.3%的干净性能；在DMControl泛化任务（随机颜色和视频背景扰动）上达到SOTA结果。

Conclusion: ACO-MoE通过解耦扰动与任务相关特征，显著提升了视觉RL在动态视觉扰动下的鲁棒性，证明了感知解耦策略的有效性。

Abstract: Visual reinforcement learning aims to empower an agent to learn policies from visual observations, yet it remains vulnerable to dynamic visual perturbations, such as unpredictable shifts in corruption types. To systematically study this, we introduce the Visual Degraded Control Suite (VDCS), a benchmark extending DeepMind Control Suite with Markov-switching degradations to simulate non-stationary real-world perturbations. Experiments on VDCS reveal severe performance degradation in existing methods. We theoretically prove via information-theoretic analysis that this failure stems from reconstruction-based objectives inevitably entangling perturbation artifacts into latent representations. To mitigate this negative impact, we propose Agent-Centric Observations with Mixture-of-Experts (ACO-MoE) to robustify visual RL against perturbations. The proposed framework leverages unique agent-centric restoration experts, achieving restoration from corruptions and task-relevant foreground extraction, thereby decoupling perception from perturbation before being processed by the RL agent. Extensive experiments on VDCS show our ACO-MoE outperforms strong baselines, recovering 95.3% of clean performance under challenging Markov-switching corruptions. Moreover, it achieves SOTA results on DMControl Generalization with random-color and video-background perturbations, demonstrating a high level of robustness.

</details>


### [387] [Pushing Radar Odometry Beyond the Pavement: Current Capabilities and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24674)
*Shaunak Kolhe,Peng Jiang,Maggie Wigness,Philip Osteen,Timothy Overbye,Chrisitan Ellis,Srikanth Saripalli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了雷达在非结构化越野环境中的里程计估计潜力，发现全SE(3)车辆运动、地形地面回波和稀疏不稳定特征是主要挑战，并提出两种简单基线方法：Radar-KISSICP（运动补偿+3D点云）和Radar-IMU（IMU预积分辅助扫描匹配），在GO数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达在越野环境中的里程计性能尚未被充分研究，现有工作多聚焦于城市平面场景，而越野场景中的全SE(3)运动、地形地面回波和稀疏特征等问题亟待探索。

Method: 提出两种基线方法：1) Radar-KISSICP，通过运动补偿生成3D感知雷达点云；2) Radar-IMU，利用IMU预积分稳定扫描匹配。

Result: 在Great Outdoors (GO)数据集上的实验表明，所提基线方法在具有挑战性的越野路线上改善了轨迹估计性能。

Conclusion: 所提基线为越野环境中雷达里程计的未来发展提供了参考基准，验证了雷达在非结构化环境中的潜力。

Abstract: Radar offers unique advantages for localization in unstructured environments, including robustness to weather, lighting, and airborne particulates. While most prior work has studied radar odometry in urban, largely planar settings, its performance in off-road environments remains less understood. In this paper, we investigate the potential of radar for off-road odometry estimation and identify key challenges that arise from full $SE(3)$ vehicle motion, terrain-induced ground returns, and sparse or unstable features. To address these issues, we introduce two simple baselines: Radar-KISSICP, which applies motion compensation to generate 3D-aware radar pointclouds, and Radar-IMU, which leverages IMU preintegration to stabilize scan matching. Experiments on the Great Outdoors (GO) dataset demonstrate that these baselines improve trajectory estimation in challenging routes and provide a reference point for future development of radar odometry in off-road robotics.

</details>


### [388] [Learning Human-Intention Priors from Large-Scale Human Demonstrations for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24681)
*Yifan Xie,YuAn Wang,Guangyu Chen,Jinkun Liu,Yu Sun,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoT-HRA是一个层次化视觉-语言-动作框架，通过从大规模人类演示中学习意图先验，将人类视频中的操作先验用于机器人学习。它构建了HA-2.2M数据集（220万条动作-语言数据），并设计了三个耦合专家模块（视觉-语言专家、意图专家、精细专家），通过共享注意力和只读键值传输实现鲁棒控制。实验验证了其在手部运动生成、模拟操作和真实机器人任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频包含丰富的操作先验，但直接将其用于机器人学习存在困难，因为原始观测数据将场景理解、人体运动和具体化动作纠缠在一起，难以解耦迁移。

Method: 提出MoT-HRA框架，包含：(1) HA-2.2M数据集，通过手部中心过滤、空间重建、时间分割和语言对齐从异构人类视频中构建；(2) 三个耦合专家：视觉-语言专家预测与具身无关的3D轨迹，意图专家建模MANO风格的手部运动作为潜在人体运动先验，精细专家将意图感知表示映射到机器人动作块；(3) 共享注意力主干和只读键值传输机制。

Result: 在手部运动生成、模拟操作和真实机器人任务中，MoT-HRA在运动合理性和分布偏移下的鲁棒控制方面均取得改进。

Conclusion: MoT-HRA通过层次化解耦人类操作先验，有效弥合了人类演示与机器人学习之间的鸿沟，提升了机器人操作的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Human videos contain rich manipulation priors, but using them for robot learning remains difficult because raw observations entangle scene understanding, human motion, and embodiment-specific action. We introduce MoT-HRA, a hierarchical vision-language-action framework that learns human-intention priors from large-scale human demonstrations. We first curate HA-2.2M, a 2.2M-episode action-language dataset reconstructed from heterogeneous human videos through hand-centric filtering, spatial reconstruction, temporal segmentation, and language alignment. On top of this dataset, MoT-HRA factorizes manipulation into three coupled experts: a vision-language expert predicts an embodiment-agnostic 3D trajectory, an intention expert models MANO-style hand motion as a latent human-motion prior, and a fine expert maps the intention-aware representation to robot action chunks. A shared-attention trunk and read-only key-value transfer allow downstream control to use human priors while limiting interference with upstream representations. Experiments on hand motion generation, simulated manipulation, and real-world robot tasks show that MoT-HRA improves motion plausibility and robust control under distribution shift.

</details>


### [389] [Passage-Aware Structural Mapping for RGB-D Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24707)
*Ali Tourani,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Saad Ejaz,David Pérez Saura,Asier Bikandi-Noya,Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez,Holger Voos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向RGB-D VSLAM的通道感知结构建图方法，通过融合几何、语义和拓扑线索检测门与可穿越开口，将门建模为嵌入墙面的平面实体并基于共面性分类，通过遍历证据和几何开口验证推断通道，集成到vS-Graphs中增强房间连通性建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 门和通道是室内机器人导航的关键结构元素，但在现代VSLAM框架中尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 联合融合几何、语义和拓扑线索检测门与可穿越开口；将门建模为嵌入墙面的平面实体，基于与支撑墙的共面性分类为可穿越或不可穿越；通过两种互补策略推断通道：连续关键帧间相机-墙面交互的遍历证据积累，以及基于建图墙面几何不连续性的几何开口验证。

Result: 在室内办公序列上的定性评估展示了可靠的门洞检测能力。

Conclusion: 该方法丰富了场景图的通道级抽象，改进了房间连通性建模，为BIM-informed VSLAM中利用这些元素奠定了基础。

Abstract: Doorways and passages are critical structural elements for indoor robot navigation, yet they remain underexplored in modern Visual SLAM (VSLAM) frameworks. This paper presents a passage-aware structural mapping approach for RGB-D VSLAM that detects doors and traversable openings by jointly fusing geometric, semantic, and topological cues. Doors are modeled as planar entities embedded within walls and classified as traversable or non-traversable based on their coplanarity with the supporting wall. Passages are inferred through two complementary strategies: traversal evidence accumulated from camera-wall interactions across consecutive keyframes, and geometric opening validation based on discontinuities in the mapped wall geometry. The proposed method is integrated into vS-Graphs as a proof of concept, enriching its scene graph with passage-level abstractions and improving room connectivity modeling. Qualitative evaluations on indoor office sequences demonstrate reliable doorway detection, and the framework lays the foundation for exploiting these elements in BIM-informed VSLAM. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/snt-arg/visual_sgraphs/tree/doorway_integration.

</details>
